        function __lqV7DrawObject(g, q) {

        function __lqV7Polish(g, spec) {
          // Pixel-art shadows and rim lights. Geometry is baked once into a reusable room texture.
          const w = spec.w * 32,
            h = spec.h * 32;
          g.fillStyle(0x0c1220, 0.1).fillRect(6, 64, w - 12, 7);
          for (const item of spec.items) {
            if (__lqV7ObjectHints[item.k] || ['stove', 'fireplace'].includes(item.k)) {
              if (item.k === 'fireplace' || item.k === 'stove' || item.k === 'lantern') {
                const col = item.k === 'lantern' ? 0xffd889 : 0xff9d4e;
                g.fillStyle(col, 0.08).fillEllipse(
                  (item.x + item.w / 2) * 32,
                  (item.y + item.h / 2) * 32,
                  100,
                  72,
                );
              }
            }
            if (
              ['partition', 'crystal', 'forge', 'orepile', 'glasscase', 'rack', 'lantern'].includes(
                item.k,
              )
            )
              __lqV7DrawObject(g, item);
          }
          g.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.03).fillRect(8, 70, w - 16, 1);
        }
        function __lqV8Decor(e, t) {
          const W = t.w * 32,
            H = t.h * 32;
          e.lineStyle(2, 0x31272a, 0.16).strokeRect(8, 68, W - 16, H - 76);
          for (let x = 18; x < W - 18; x += 48) {
            e.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.05).fillRect(x, 61, 13, 2);
            e.fillStyle(0x1c2730, 0.13).fillRect(x + 3, 66, 7, 3);
          }
          const lit = new Set([
            'lantern',
            'forge',
            'fireplace',
            'crystal',
            'stove',
            'tv',
            'cakecase',
          ]);
          for (const item of t.items || []) {
            if (!lit.has(item.k)) continue;
            const cx = (item.x + item.w / 2) * 32,
              cy = (item.y + item.h / 2) * 32;
            const gold = ['forge', 'fireplace', 'stove', 'cakecase'].includes(item.k),
              col = gold ? 0xffb65d : 0x8adfff;
            // Pixel-style light pools, lower alpha keeps existing furniture completely visible.
            e.fillStyle(col, 0.035).fillCircle(cx, cy, Math.max(25, item.w * 23));
            e.fillStyle(col, 0.095).fillCircle(cx, cy, Math.max(10, item.w * 9));
          }
        }

        /* ===== LIFEQUEST V9 • CINEMATIC PIXEL GRAPHICS =====
   Original world architecture, gameplay, maps, and collision data are unchanged.
   Decorative geometry is painted once per texture; ambient lights use pooled Phaser objects. */
        const LQ9 = (() => {
          'use strict';
          const PI = Math.PI,
            TERRAIN_SEED = 0x9e3779b9;
          const hash = (x, y, z = 0) => {
            let n =
              (Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) +
                Math.imul(y | 0, 668265263) +
                Math.imul((z | 0) ^ TERRAIN_SEED, 2147483647)) |
              0;
            n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177);
            return ((n ^ (n >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;
          };
          const col = (g, c, a = 1) => g.fillStyle(c, a);
          const rect = (g, x, y, w, h, c, a = 1) => g.fillStyle(c, a).fillRect(x, y, w, h);
          const line = (g, x1, y1, x2, y2, c, a = 1, w = 1) =>
            g.lineStyle(w, c, a).lineBetween(x1, y1, x2, y2);
          const oval = (g, x, y, w, h, c, a = 1) => g.fillStyle(c, a).fillEllipse(x, y, w, h);
          const dot = (g, c, x, y, radius = 2, alpha = 1) => g.fillStyle(c, alpha).fillCircle(x, y, radius);
          const pointIn = (x, y, b) => x >= b.x && x < b.x + b.w && y >= b.y && y < b.y + b.h;
          const tileRoad = (a, x, y) => typeof a.isRoad === 'function' && a.isRoad(x, y);
          const avoid = (a, x, y) =>
            a.buildings.some((b) =>
              pointIn(x, y, { x: b.x - 1, y: b.y - 1, w: b.w + 2, h: b.h + 2 }),
            ) || a.obstacles.some((o) => pointIn(x, y, o));
          const mine = (a) => a.theme === 'mine';
          function drawTerrain(g, a) {
            const mineral = mine(a),
              W = a.w,
              H = a.h;
            // Hand-painted looking ground micro-details and patterned cobblestone edging.
            for (let y = 1; y < H - 1; y++)
              for (let x = 1; x < W - 1; x++) {
                const p = hash(x, y, 3);
                if (avoid(a, x, y)) continue;
                const px = x * 32,
                  py = y * 32;
                if (tileRoad(a, x, y)) {
                  if (p > 0.84) {
                    rect(g, px + 8, py + 8, 10, 1, mineral ? 0xe5d3a4 : 0xc4c8d0, 0.12);
                    rect(g, px + 18, py + 21, 6, 1, 0x19202e, 0.18);
                  }
                  if (p < 0.055) {
                    oval(g, px + 16, py + 15, 10, 7, 0x1f2931, 0.2);
                    line(g, px + 13, py + 15, px + 19, py + 15, 0xdce2e5, 0.17);
                  }
                } else if (p > 0.66) {
                  rect(
                    g,
                    px + 6 + (((p * 18) | 0) % 11),
                    py + 11,
                    3,
                    2,
                    mineral ? 0xc3d1c7 : 0xd0e3b1,
                    0.17,
                  );
                  rect(
                    g,
                    px + 22,
                    py + 5 + (((p * 13) | 0) % 9),
                    1,
                    4,
                    mineral ? 0x84dfca : 0x365e3a,
                    0.28,
                  );
                  if (p > 0.89) {
                    rect(g, px + 10, py + 23, 2, 3, mineral ? 0xa4e9dc : 0xffe0ac, 0.53);
                    rect(g, px + 11, py + 22, 1, 1, 0xffffff, 0.6);
                  }
                }
              }
            // Architecturally useful entry paths: no new colliders, drawn beneath original buildings.
            for (const b of a.buildings) {
              let px = b.door * 32 + 16,
                py = (b.y + b.h) * 32;
              for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                let y = py + 9 + k * 13;
                if (y > H * 32 - 8) break;
                rect(g, px - 20, y, 40, 11, mineral ? 0xa79a82 : 0xdccbad, 0.48);
                rect(g, px - 20, y + 9, 40, 2, 0x3b3943, 0.2);
                line(g, px, y, px, y + 10, 0xffffff, 0.09);
              }
            }
            if (!mineral) {
              // Small flower-beds near street corners (decorative only).
              for (let y = 3; y < H - 3; y += 4)
                for (let x = 3; x < W - 3; x += 4) {
                  const k = hash(x, y, 95);
                  if (k < 0.68 || avoid(a, x, y) || tileRoad(a, x, y)) continue;
                  let cx = x * 32 + 14,
                    cy = y * 32 + 12;
                  oval(g, cx, cy + 3, 24, 11, 0x235c37, 0.24);
                  for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
                    const ox = j * 7 - 6,
                      colors = [0xffcfd1, 0xffcf77, 0xcef6ff];
                    oval(g, cx + ox, cy - 2 - (j % 2) * 4, 5, 5, colors[j], 0.8);
                    rect(g, cx + ox, cy + 2, 1, 5, 0x275c31, 0.73);
                  }
                }
            } else {
              // Subtle ore shimmer and mineral fault lines, anchored to non-colliding floor tiles.
              for (let y = 2; y < H - 2; y += 2)
                for (let x = 2; x < W - 2; x += 2) {
                  let q = hash(x, y, 48);
                  if (q < 0.73 || avoid(a, x, y) || tileRoad(a, x, y)) continue;
                  let cx = x * 32 + 15,
                    cy = y * 32 + 8;
                  line(g, cx - 10, cy + 13, cx, cy + 8, 0x485d6c, 0.44, 1);
                  line(g, cx, cy + 8, cx + 8, cy + 10, 0x5ac3bc, 0.36, 1);
                  rect(g, cx + 1, cy + 6, 3, 3, 0x8eeedc, 0.56);
                }
            }
          }
          function drawLandscape(g, a) {
            for (const o of a.obstacles) {
              let x = o.x * 32,
                y = o.y * 32;
              if (o.kind === 'tree' || o.kind === 'pine') {
                oval(g, x + 17, y + 8, 14, 8, 0xe9ffc4, 0.17);
                rect(g, x + 10, y + 6, 3, 2, 0xffffff, 0.22);
                rect(g, x + 23, y + 11, 3, 2, 0xb4f5c1, 0.23);
              } else if (o.kind === 'pond' || o.kind === 'fountain') {
                for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                  const yy = y + 9 + i * 10;
                  line(g, x + 9 + (i % 2) * 8, yy, x + 19 + (i % 2) * 8, yy, 0xd8fdff, 0.28);
                }
              } else if (o.kind === 'rock' && mine(a)) {
                rect(g, x + 9, y + 8, 5, 2, 0xc9edf1, 0.12);
              } else if (o.kind === 'lamp') {
                oval(g, x + 16, y + 3, 24, 13, 0xffe5a1, 0.1);
              }
            }
          }
          function drawBuilding(g, b, a) {
            const x = b.x * 32,
              y = b.y * 32,
              w = b.w * 32,
              h = b.h * 32;
            const roof = Math.round(h * ((b.floors ?? 1) === 2 ? 0.4 : 0.55));
            const mineral = mine(a),
              gold = mineral ? 0xffda9d : 0xffefbe;
            const name = (b.name || '').toLowerCase();
            const isHouse = /house|home/.test(name),
              isShop = /shop|market|cafe|restaurant|hotel|inn/.test(name),
              isCivic = /bank|hall|school|library|hospital|clinic|post|station/.test(name),
              isMine = /mine|smithy|tool/.test(name);
            const nameHash = [...(b.name || 'house')].reduce(
              (s, ch) => ((s * 33) ^ (ch.charCodeAt(0) + 17)) >>> 0,
              5381,
            );
            const seed =
              (((b.x + 3) * 92837111) ^
                ((b.y + 7) * 689287499) ^
                ((b.w + 5) * 283923481) ^
                ((b.h + 11) * 1199540899) ^
                nameHash) >>>
              0;
            const houseVariant = seed % 18,
              palette = (seed >>> 4) % 8,
              detail = (seed >>> 8) % 7,
              civicVariant = (seed >>> 11) % 8,
              shopVariant = (seed >>> 14) % 8;
            const wallStart = y + roof;

            const district = (() => {
              if (mineral) return 'mineral';
              if (y < 320 && x < 480) return 'old';
              if (y < 320 && x >= 480) return 'wealthy';
              if (y >= 320 && x < 480) return 'green';
              return 'modern';
            })();
            const districtMood = {
              old: {
                roofShift: 0xb26f4f,

                leaf: 0x5ca292,
                accentMix: 0x547f9f,
              },
              mineral: {
                roofShift: 0x55717e,
                trimBoost: 0xd8f8f4,

                accentMix: 0x5a9090,
              },
            }[district];

            const palettes = [
              {
                accent: 0x6b7ea6,
                trim: 0xbad8ef,

                flower: 0xeb8484,
                leaf: 0x5cb56b,

              },

                roofA: 0x5a7f65,
              },
              {
                accent: 0x936d54,
                trim: 0xf2dcc7,

                box: 0x8d5f5f,
                flower: 0xc6a5ff,
                leaf: 0x6ea869,
                roofA: 0x70567a,
              },
              {
                accent: 0x5a8d9b,
                trim: 0xd5eef4,
                box: 0x8a6b50,
                flower: 0xffa68f,
                leaf: 0x58aa84,
                roofA: 0x528090,
              },
              {
                accent: 0x9a7351,
                trim: 0xf6e2b6,
                box: 0x7b5234,
                flower: 0xffc86c,
                leaf: 0x6ab072,
                roofA: 0x886142,
              },
              {
                accent: 0x5f7396,
                trim: 0xd7e1f6,
                box: 0x806151,
                flower: 0xf5a3a3,
                leaf: 0x61af73,
                roofA: 0x4c5e86,
              },
              {
                accent: 0x7a8451,
                trim: 0xecf1cd,
                box: 0x866343,
                flower: 0xffde7f,
                leaf: 0x76b06e,
                roofA: 0x6f7b47,
              },
            ][palette];
            const pal = {
              accent: mineral ? 0x5ca8a3 : (palettes.accent + districtMood.accentMix) >>> 1,
              trim: mineral ? 0xd9faf4 : (palettes.trim * 0.55 + districtMood.trimBoost * 0.45) | 0,
              box: palettes.box,
              flower: palettes.flower,
              leaf: mineral ? 0x63c5b6 : (palettes.leaf + districtMood.leaf) >>> 1,
              roofA: mineral ? 0x56737c : (palettes.roofA + districtMood.roofShift) >>> 1,
            };

            const roofBand = (yy, a = 0.1) => {
              for (let xx = x + 10 + ((((yy - y) / 18) % 2) | 0) * 9; xx < x + w - 12; xx += 27) {
                rect(g, xx, yy, 12, 2, pal.roofA, a);
                rect(g, xx + 2, yy + 3, 3, 1, 0xffffff, a * 0.85);
              }
            };
            const lineH = (yy, c, a = 0.15, wid = 1) =>
              line(g, x + 7, yy, x + w - 7, yy, c, a, wid);
            const drawDormer = (
              px,
              py,
              wide = 16,
              tall = 10,
              color = 0xaadbf4,
              frame = pal.accent,
            ) => {
              rect(g, px - wide / 2 - 3, py - 5, wide + 6, tall + 8, frame, 0.2);
              rect(g, px - wide / 2 - 1, py - 3, wide + 2, tall + 3, 0x3a2f36, 0.22);
              rect(g, px - wide / 2, py - 2, wide, tall, color, 0.5);
              line(g, px, py - 2, px, py + tall - 1, 0x4e3f44, 0.7, 1);
            };
            const drawRoundWindow = (px, py, r = 8) => {
              oval(g, px, py, r * 2 + 2, r * 2 + 2, 0x2b2a35, 0.15);
              oval(g, px, py, r * 2, r * 2, 0xbbe5fb, 0.48);
              oval(g, px, py, r * 1.2, r * 1.2, 0xffffff, 0.18);
            };
            const drawFlowerBox = (px, py, size = 20) => {
              rect(g, px - size / 2, py, size, 5, pal.box, 0.86);
              for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++)
                oval(
                  g,
                  px + k * (size / 4),
                  py - 5 - ((k + 3) % 2 ? 0 : 3),
                  7,
                  8,
                  [pal.flower, 0xffd36b, 0xf0a2db][(k + 3) % 3],
                  0.76,
                );
            };
            const drawShutters = (px, py) => {
              rect(g, px - 13, py, 4, 14, pal.accent, 0.55);
              rect(g, px + 9, py, 4, 14, pal.accent, 0.55);
              rect(g, px - 8, py + 2, 16, 10, 0xc8ebff, 0.3);
            };
            const drawPorch = (px, width = 38) => {
              rect(g, px - width / 2, y + h - 46, width, 4, pal.accent, 0.25);
              line(
                g,
                px - width / 2 + 2,
                y + h - 42,
                px + width / 2 - 2,
                y + h - 42,
                0xffffff,
                0.16,
                1,
              );
              rect(g, px - width / 2 + 4, y + h - 42, 3, 8, pal.trim, 0.22);
              rect(g, px + width / 2 - 7, y + h - 42, 3, 8, pal.trim, 0.22);
            };
            const drawVines = (px, steps = 5) => {
              for (let k = 0; k < steps; k++)
                oval(g, px + (k % 2) * 4, y + h - 35 - k * 4, 7, 8, pal.leaf, 0.48);
            };
            const drawBeamPanel = (x1, y1, w1, h1) => {
              rect(g, x1, y1, w1, h1, pal.accent, 0.1);
              line(g, x1, y1, x1 + w1, y1, 0x2d2530, 0.22, 1);
              line(g, x1, y1 + h1, x1 + w1, y1 + h1, 0xffffff, 0.08, 1);
            };
            const drawMiniChimney = (px, py, tall = 14) => {
              rect(g, px - 6, py, 12, tall, detail % 2 ? 0x6e5b66 : 0x76665f, 0.86);
              rect(g, px - 4, py - 9, 8, 9, 0xd9dfeb, 0.56);
              rect(g, px - 2, py - 7, 4, 4, 0xffffff, 0.18);
              line(g, px - 6, py, px + 6, py, 0x2c2430, 0.45, 1);
            };
            const drawCanopy = (px, py, width = 46) => {
              rect(g, px - width / 2, py, width, 5, pal.accent, 0.18);
              for (let i = 0; i < width; i += 8)
                rect(g, px - width / 2 + i, py, 4, 5, i % 16 === 0 ? pal.trim : 0xffffff, 0.1);
            };
            const drawColumn = (px, top, bottom) => {
              rect(g, px - 4, top, 8, bottom - top, pal.trim, 0.22);
              rect(g, px - 6, top - 2, 12, 3, pal.accent, 0.18);
              rect(g, px - 6, bottom, 12, 3, pal.accent, 0.16);
            };
            const drawBanner = (px, py, height = 24) => {
              rect(g, px - 2, py, 4, height, 0x5d504e, 0.45);
              rect(g, px + 2, py + 4, 12, height - 6, pal.accent, 0.34);
              rect(g, px + 4, py + 8, 8, 2, pal.trim, 0.28);
            };
            const drawLantern = (px, py) => {
              rect(g, px - 1, py, 2, 10, 0x4e453b, 0.5);
              oval(g, px, py + 11, 10, 12, 0xffe6a8, 0.28);
              rect(g, px - 3, py + 7, 6, 6, 0xffedc0, 0.58);
            };
            const drawLargeWindow = (px, py, width = 20, height = 14) => {
              rect(g, px - width / 2, py, width, height, 0xc8ebff, 0.28);
              line(g, px, py, px, py + height, 0x4e3f44, 0.5, 1);
              line(
                g,
                px - width / 2,
                py + height / 2,
                px + width / 2,
                py + height / 2,
                0x4e3f44,
                0.45,
                1,
              );
            };
            const drawMedallion = (px, py, r = 8) => {
              oval(g, px, py, r * 2 + 2, r * 2 + 2, 0x2b2a35, 0.12);
              oval(g, px, py, r * 2, r * 2, pal.trim, 0.28);
              oval(g, px, py, r * 1.1, r * 1.1, pal.accent, 0.2);
            };

            const drawClockFace = (px, py, r = 8) => {
              oval(g, px, py, r * 2 + 2, r * 2 + 2, 0x223042, 0.18);
              oval(g, px, py, r * 2, r * 2, 0xf3f1e2, 0.7);
              oval(g, px, py, r * 1.65, r * 1.65, 0x30465c, 0.28);
              line(g, px, py, px, py - r + 3, 0x394555, 0.9, 1);
              line(g, px, py, px + r - 3, py + 1, 0x394555, 0.9, 1);
            };
            const drawPediment = (px, py, width = 46, height = 13, color = pal.accent) => {
              g.fillStyle(color, 0.24).fillTriangle(px - width / 2, py + height, px + width / 2, py + height, px, py);
              line(g, px - width / 2, py + height, px + width / 2, py + height, 0xffffff, 0.12, 1);
            };
            const drawGarageDoor = (px, py, width = 24, height = 22, stripe = 0xcad6df) => {
              rect(g, px - width / 2, py, width, height, 0x34414d, 0.82);
              rect(g, px - width / 2 + 2, py + 2, width - 4, height - 4, 0x161e28, 0.42);
              for (let yy = py + 3; yy < py + height - 2; yy += 4) line(g, px - width / 2 + 3, yy, px + width / 2 - 3, yy, stripe, 0.32, 1);
            };
            const drawMarquee = (px, py, width = 60, height = 14, acc = 0xffd447) => {
              rect(g, px - width / 2, py, width, height, 0x4f3942, 0.78);
              rect(g, px - width / 2 + 3, py + 3, width - 6, height - 6, 0x191d2b, 0.56);
              for (let xx = px - width / 2 + 5; xx <= px + width / 2 - 5; xx += 8) dot(g, acc, xx, py + height - 2, 1.5, 0.82);
            };
            const drawCrossEmblem = (px, py, size = 8) => {
              rect(g, px - size / 2, py - size * 1.15, size, size * 2.3, 0xffffff, 0.72);
              rect(g, px - size * 1.15, py - size / 2, size * 2.3, size, 0xffffff, 0.72);
              rect(g, px - size / 2 + 1, py - size * 1.15 + 1, size - 2, size * 2.3 - 2, 0xd36363, 0.74);
              rect(g, px - size * 1.15 + 1, py - size / 2 + 1, size * 2.3 - 2, size - 2, 0xd36363, 0.74);
            };
            const drawGlassCanopy = (px, py, width = 44) => {
              rect(g, px - width / 2, py, width, 5, 0x77b4d4, 0.26);
              line(g, px - width / 2, py + 5, px + width / 2, py + 5, 0xffffff, 0.18, 1);
              rect(g, px - width / 2 + 5, py + 5, 2, 8, pal.trim, 0.16);
              rect(g, px + width / 2 - 7, py + 5, 2, 8, pal.trim, 0.16);
            };

            // district-themed subtle ground/facade cues
            if (district === 'wealthy') {
              rect(g, x + 10, wallStart + 8, w - 20, 3, 0xfff4d7, 0.08);
            }
            if (district === 'old') {
              for (let xx = x + 12; xx < x + w - 12; xx += 16)
                rect(g, xx, wallStart + 10, 6, 1, 0x7f604b, 0.1);
            }
            if (district === 'modern') {
              rect(g, x + 14, wallStart + 11, w - 28, 2, 0xffffff, 0.08);
            }
            if (district === 'green') {
              for (let xx = x + 12; xx < x + w - 12; xx += 22)
                oval(g, xx, wallStart + 11, 6, 5, pal.leaf, 0.16);
            }

            // Roof base and texture.
            rect(g, x + 7, y + 3, w - 14, 3, gold, 0.18);
            line(g, x + 7, y + 6, x + w - 7, y + 6, 0x181f2e, 0.24);
            for (let yy = y + 13; yy < y + roof - 7; yy += 18) roofBand(yy, isHouse ? 0.09 : 0.12);

            const drawHouseVariant = () => {
              if (houseVariant === 0 || houseVariant === 4 || houseVariant === 12) {
                lineH(y + roof - 9, pal.accent, 0.18, 2);
              }
              if (houseVariant === 1 || houseVariant === 7 || houseVariant === 13) {
                for (let xx = x + 16; xx < x + w - 15; xx += 18)
                  rect(g, xx, y + roof - 12, 8, 2, pal.trim, 0.16);
              }
              if (houseVariant === 2 || houseVariant === 8 || houseVariant === 14) {
                for (let xx = x + 14; xx < x + w - 14; xx += 22)
                  line(g, xx, y + 9, xx + 10, y + roof - 10, pal.accent, 0.09, 1);
              }
              if (houseVariant === 3 || houseVariant === 9 || houseVariant === 15) {
                rect(g, x + 12, y + roof - 15, w - 24, 4, pal.accent, 0.12);
              }
              if (
                houseVariant === 5 ||
                houseVariant === 6 ||
                houseVariant === 16 ||
                houseVariant === 17
              ) {
                rect(g, x + w * 0.18, y + roof - 14, w * 0.64, 3, pal.trim, 0.14);
              }

              switch (houseVariant) {
                case 0:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.24), y + Math.round(roof * 0.23));
                  drawDormer(
                    x + Math.round(w * 0.69),
                    y + Math.round(roof * 0.43),
                    16,
                    9,
                    0xaadbf4,
                  );
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.28, y + h - 26, 18);
                  drawBeamPanel(x + 12, wallStart + 13, w - 24, 10);
                  break;
                case 1:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.76), y + Math.round(roof * 0.23), 16);
                  drawRoundWindow(x + Math.round(w * 0.5), y + Math.round(roof * 0.45), 7);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.28, y + h - 40);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.72, y + h - 40);
                  drawCanopy(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 47, 42);
                  break;
                case 2:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.17), y + Math.round(roof * 0.22), 13);
                  drawDormer(
                    x + Math.round(w * 0.32),
                    y + Math.round(roof * 0.43),
                    14,
                    8,
                    0xb5e9ff,
                  );
                  drawDormer(
                    x + Math.round(w * 0.68),
                    y + Math.round(roof * 0.43),
                    14,
                    8,
                    0xb5e9ff,
                  );
                  for (let yy = wallStart + 12; yy < y + h - 16; yy += 8)
                    rect(g, x + 12, yy, w - 24, 1, 0xffffff, 0.06);
                  break;
                case 3:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.73), y + Math.round(roof * 0.25), 15);
                  drawPorch(x + w * 0.5, 42);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.22, y + h - 26, 18);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.78, y + h - 26, 18);
                  drawVines(x + 14, 4);
                  break;
                case 4:
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 16, y + Math.round(roof * 0.28), 32, 20, pal.accent, 0.16);
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.5, y + Math.round(roof * 0.46), 6);
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.22), y + Math.round(roof * 0.22), 14);
                  drawBeamPanel(x + w * 0.5 - 18, wallStart + 11, 36, 13);
                  break;
                case 5:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.72), y + Math.round(roof * 0.22), 14);
                  drawDormer(
                    x + Math.round(w * 0.72),
                    y + Math.round(roof * 0.38),
                    18,
                    10,
                    0xc3ecff,
                  );
                  drawVines(x + 12, 6);
                  drawVines(x + w - 26, 6);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 26, 22);
                  break;
                case 6:
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 18, y + Math.round(roof * 0.38), 36, 8, 0x253748, 0.32);
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 14, y + Math.round(roof * 0.4), 28, 4, 0xb9e7fc, 0.28);
                  drawPorch(x + w * 0.5, 34);
                  rect(g, x + 16, wallStart + 13, w - 32, 12, pal.accent, 0.08);
                  break;
                case 7:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.26), y + Math.round(roof * 0.22), 15);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.3, y + h - 40);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.7, y + h - 40);
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 10, y + h - 24, 20, 2, pal.accent, 0.32);
                  for (let xx = x + 14; xx < x + w - 14; xx += 18)
                    rect(g, xx, wallStart + 12, 3, 14, 0x6b4a36, 0.12);
                  break;
                case 8:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.22), y + Math.round(roof * 0.18), 18);
                  drawDormer(x + Math.round(w * 0.7), y + Math.round(roof * 0.44), 14, 8, 0xcbeeff);
                  drawBeamPanel(x + 14, wallStart + 13, w * 0.38, 12);
                  drawCanopy(x + w * 0.67, y + h - 47, 34);
                  break;
                case 9:
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.34, y + Math.round(roof * 0.45), 6);
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.66, y + Math.round(roof * 0.45), 6);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 26, 22);
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.78), y + Math.round(roof * 0.22), 13);
                  rect(g, x + 18, wallStart + 12, w - 36, 3, pal.trim, 0.12);
                  break;
                case 10:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.48), y + Math.round(roof * 0.2), 16);
                  drawDormer(
                    x + Math.round(w * 0.28),
                    y + Math.round(roof * 0.42),
                    12,
                    8,
                    0xcaecff,
                  );
                  drawDormer(
                    x + Math.round(w * 0.72),
                    y + Math.round(roof * 0.42),
                    12,
                    8,
                    0xcaecff,
                  );
                  drawPorch(x + w * 0.5, 46);
                  break;
                case 11:
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.5, y + Math.round(roof * 0.38), 8);
                  drawVines(x + 16, 5);
                  drawVines(x + w - 30, 4);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.3, y + h - 25, 18);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.7, y + h - 25, 18);
                  break;
                case 12:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.2), y + Math.round(roof * 0.23), 14);
                  drawBeamPanel(x + 12, wallStart + 12, w - 24, 14);
                  drawCanopy(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 48, 52);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 40);
                  break;
                case 13:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.8), y + Math.round(roof * 0.2), 17);
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.24, y + Math.round(roof * 0.34), w * 0.5, 10, pal.accent, 0.18);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.5, y + Math.round(roof * 0.39), 28, 8);
                  drawPorch(x + w * 0.5, 32);
                  break;
                case 14:
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.25, y + Math.round(roof * 0.42), 5);
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.75, y + Math.round(roof * 0.42), 5);
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.52), y + Math.round(roof * 0.21), 13);
                  for (let xx = x + 16; xx < x + w - 16; xx += 14)
                    rect(g, xx, wallStart + 14, 7, 10, pal.trim, 0.08);
                  break;
                case 15:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.24), y + Math.round(roof * 0.2), 16);
                  drawDormer(x + Math.round(w * 0.5), y + Math.round(roof * 0.4), 18, 10, 0xbfe8ff);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 24, 24);
                  drawBeamPanel(x + 16, wallStart + 15, w - 32, 8);
                  break;
                case 16:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.72), y + Math.round(roof * 0.23), 15);
                  drawCanopy(x + w * 0.33, y + h - 47, 28);
                  drawCanopy(x + w * 0.67, y + h - 47, 28);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.25, y + h - 40);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.75, y + h - 40);
                  break;
                case 17:
                  drawMiniChimney(x + Math.round(w * 0.18), y + Math.round(roof * 0.2), 18);
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 12, y + Math.round(roof * 0.3), 24, 16, pal.accent, 0.18);
                  drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.5, y + Math.round(roof * 0.44), 5);
                  drawPorch(x + w * 0.5, 40);
                  drawVines(x + w - 24, 6);
                  break;
              }

              if (detail === 0) drawBeamPanel(x + 12, wallStart + 15, w - 24, 8);
              else if (detail === 1)
                line(g, x + 12, wallStart + 17, x + w - 12, wallStart + 17, pal.trim, 0.14, 1);
              else if (detail === 2)
                for (let xx = x + 18; xx < x + w - 18; xx += 18)
                  rect(g, xx, wallStart + 14, 8, 1, pal.accent, 0.12);
              else if (detail === 3)
                for (let yy = wallStart + 14; yy < y + h - 18; yy += 9)
                  rect(g, x + 14, yy, w - 28, 1, pal.trim, 0.05);
              else if (detail === 4) drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 20, 16);
              else if (detail === 5) drawVines(x + 10 + ((seed >>> 18) % Math.max(8, w - 24)), 4);
              else if (detail === 6) drawCanopy(x + w * 0.5, y + h - 50, 26 + ((seed >>> 20) % 20));
            };

            const drawShopVariant = () => {
              const bx = x + w / 2,
                by = wallStart - 18;
              const signW = Math.max(26, Math.min(62, w * 0.36)),
                signH = 14;
              switch (shopVariant) {
                case 0:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, signH, 0x7e5338, 0.72);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2 + 2, by + 2, signW - 4, signH - 4, 0xf7e5bc, 0.34);
                  drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 9, w - 22);
                  break;
                case 1:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, signH, pal.accent, 0.62);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2 + 4, by + 3, signW - 8, signH - 6, pal.trim, 0.26);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.28, wallStart + 17, 18, 12);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.72, wallStart + 17, 18, 12);
                  break;
                case 2:
                  drawBanner(x + 16, wallStart - 10, 28);
                  drawBanner(x + w - 16, wallStart - 10, 28);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by + 2, signW, 10, 0x614437, 0.58);
                  drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 9, w - 16);
                  break;
                case 3:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 12, 0x6a4f82, 0.56);
                  drawLantern(x + 20, wallStart + 6);
                  drawLantern(x + w - 20, wallStart + 6);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.25, y + h - 25, 18);
                  drawFlowerBox(x + w * 0.75, y + h - 25, 18);
                  break;
                case 4:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 15, 0x394a62, 0.58);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.5, wallStart + 16, 28, 13);
                  drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 26);
                  break;
                case 5:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by + 1, signW, 13, 0x7a5a45, 0.64);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2 + 2, by + 3, signW - 4, 5, 0xffffff, 0.13);
                  for (let xx = x + 14; xx < x + w - 14; xx += 18) drawLantern(xx, wallStart + 6);
                  break;
                case 6:
                  drawBanner(x + 18, wallStart - 8, 24);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 14, 0x5b6c8a, 0.56);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.3, wallStart + 16, 16, 11);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.7, wallStart + 16, 16, 11);
                  drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 30);
                  break;
                case 7:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 12, 0x72563d, 0.62);
                  drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 24);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.23, y + h - 39);
                  drawShutters(x + w * 0.77, y + h - 39);
                  break;
              }
            };

            const drawCivicVariant = () => {
              const bx = x + w / 2,
                by = wallStart - 18;
              const signW = Math.max(30, Math.min(70, w * 0.4)),
                signH = 15;
              switch (civicVariant) {
                case 0:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, signH, 0x30465c, 0.68);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2 + 2, by + 2, signW - 4, signH - 4, 0xc7e7f5, 0.32);
                  drawColumn(x + 18, wallStart + 8, y + h - 14);
                  drawColumn(x + w - 18, wallStart + 8, y + h - 14);
                  break;
                case 1:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 12, 0x47515e, 0.64);
                  drawMedallion(bx, y + roof * 0.42, 7);
                  drawBanner(x + 18, wallStart - 8, 22);
                  drawBanner(x + w - 18, wallStart - 8, 22);
                  break;
                case 2:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 14, pal.accent, 0.54);
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2 + 4, by + 3, signW - 8, 5, pal.trim, 0.26);
                  for (let xx = x + 18; xx < x + w - 18; xx += 26)
                    drawColumn(xx, wallStart + 9, y + h - 15);
                  break;
                case 3:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 13, 0x40556a, 0.6);
                  drawLantern(x + 18, wallStart + 6);
                  drawLantern(x + w - 18, wallStart + 6);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.5, wallStart + 16, 24, 12);
                  break;
                case 4:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 12, 0x5d496d, 0.54);
                  drawMedallion(bx, y + roof * 0.36, 9);
                  drawColumn(x + w * 0.3, wallStart + 10, y + h - 14);
                  drawColumn(x + w * 0.7, wallStart + 10, y + h - 14);
                  break;
                case 5:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 14, 0x526a4d, 0.58);
                  drawBanner(x + 16, wallStart - 10, 26);
                  drawBanner(x + w - 16, wallStart - 10, 26);
                  drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 7, w - 30);
                  break;
                case 6:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 13, 0x6a5b42, 0.58);
                  for (let xx = x + 16; xx < x + w - 16; xx += 20) drawLantern(xx, wallStart + 5);
                  drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.5, wallStart + 15, 30, 12);
                  break;
                case 7:
                  rect(g, bx - signW / 2, by, signW, 14, 0x384c58, 0.62);
                  drawColumn(x + 20, wallStart + 8, y + h - 14);
                  drawColumn(x + w - 20, wallStart + 8, y + h - 14);
                  drawMedallion(bx, wallStart + 16, 6);
                  break;
              }
            };


            const drawSignatureFacade = () => {
              const bx = x + w / 2;
              if (b.name === 'Town Hall') {
                drawPediment(bx, wallStart - 16, 54, 14, 0x314d67);
                drawClockFace(bx, y + roof * 0.45, 9);
                drawColumn(x + 20, wallStart + 9, y + h - 14);
                drawColumn(x + w - 20, wallStart + 9, y + h - 14);
                drawBanner(x + 18, wallStart - 10, 28); drawBanner(x + w - 18, wallStart - 10, 28);
              } else if (b.name === 'Bank') {
                drawPediment(bx, wallStart - 15, 46, 12, 0x3d4b63);
                for (let xx of [x + 18, x + w * 0.5, x + w - 18]) drawColumn(xx, wallStart + 8, y + h - 14);
                drawMedallion(bx, y + roof * 0.42, 8);
              } else if (b.name === 'Library') {
                drawPediment(bx, wallStart - 14, 50, 12, 0x305864);
                drawRoundWindow(bx, y + roof * 0.43, 7);
                drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.28, wallStart + 15, 18, 12);
                drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.72, wallStart + 15, 18, 12);
              } else if (b.name === 'Hospital') {
                drawCrossEmblem(bx, y + roof * 0.45, 7);
                drawGlassCanopy(bx, y + h - 48, 58);
                drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.32, wallStart + 13, 18, 11);
                drawLargeWindow(x + w * 0.68, wallStart + 13, 18, 11);
              } else if (b.name === 'Fire Station') {
                drawGarageDoor(x + w * 0.28, y + h - 28, 24, 19);
                drawGarageDoor(x + w * 0.72, y + h - 28, 24, 19);
                drawClockFace(bx, y + roof * 0.42, 7);
              } else if (b.name === 'School') {
                drawPediment(bx, wallStart - 15, 68, 12, 0x39576a);
                drawClockFace(bx, y + roof * 0.44, 8);
                drawBanner(x + 20, wallStart - 8, 26); drawBanner(x + w - 20, wallStart - 8, 26);
                drawColumn(x + w * 0.28, wallStart + 10, y + h - 14); drawColumn(x + w * 0.72, wallStart + 10, y + h - 14);
              } else if (b.name === 'Hotel' || b.name === 'Mining Inn') {
                drawMarquee(bx, wallStart - 16, Math.min(70, w - 18), 13, 0xffdf82);
                drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 24);
                drawLantern(x + 20, wallStart + 6); drawLantern(x + w - 20, wallStart + 6);
              } else if (b.name === 'Cinema') {
                drawMarquee(bx, wallStart - 16, Math.min(78, w - 12), 15, 0xffd447);
                drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 22);
                drawRoundWindow(bx, y + roof * 0.42, 7);
              } else if (b.name === 'Church') {
                drawPediment(bx, wallStart - 15, 34, 10, 0x4b5b72);
                drawRoundWindow(bx, y + roof * 0.44, 7);
                drawLantern(x + w * 0.5, wallStart + 6);
              } else if (b.name === 'Sunny Cafe' || b.name === 'Restaurant' || b.name === 'Market' || b.name === 'Corner Shop') {
                drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 18);
                drawLantern(x + 18, wallStart + 7); drawLantern(x + w - 18, wallStart + 7);
              } else if (b.name === 'Great Mine') {
                rect(g, bx - 26, y + roof * 0.24, 8, 42, 0x48545e, 0.86);
                rect(g, bx + 18, y + roof * 0.24, 8, 42, 0x48545e, 0.86);
                line(g, bx - 22, y + roof * 0.25, bx, y + roof * 0.06, 0x8e9aa1, 0.8, 3);
                line(g, bx + 22, y + roof * 0.25, bx, y + roof * 0.06, 0x8e9aa1, 0.8, 3);
                drawClockFace(bx, y + roof * 0.18, 6);
              } else if (b.name === 'Rail Station') {
                drawClockFace(bx, y + roof * 0.42, 7);
                drawGlassCanopy(bx, y + h - 47, 62);
                drawLantern(x + 20, wallStart + 6); drawLantern(x + w - 20, wallStart + 6);
              } else if (b.name === 'Color Gallery' || b.name === 'Open Canvas Gallery') {
                drawMarquee(bx, wallStart - 16, Math.min(72, w - 18), 13, 0xf48cc4);
                drawLargeWindow(bx, wallStart + 14, 34, 12);
              } else if (b.name === 'Backbeat Warehouse') {
                drawMarquee(bx, wallStart - 14, Math.min(66, w - 14), 12, 0x62d1c5);
                drawGarageDoor(bx, y + h - 28, 32, 19, 0x88a2b6);
              } else if (b.name === 'Photo Lab') {
                drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.3, y + roof * 0.44, 6);
                drawRoundWindow(x + w * 0.7, y + roof * 0.44, 6);
                drawGlassCanopy(bx, y + h - 47, 48);
              } else if (b.name === 'Mural Workshop' || b.name === 'Independent Studio') {
                drawBanner(x + 16, wallStart - 9, 26); drawBanner(x + w - 16, wallStart - 9, 26);
                drawCanopy(bx, wallStart + 8, w - 24);
              }
            };

            if (isHouse && b.w >= 4) {
              drawHouseVariant();
            } else if (
              (isShop || isCivic) &&
              b.w >= 5 &&
              b.name !== 'Great Mine' &&
              b.name !== 'School'
            ) {
              if (isShop) drawShopVariant();
              else drawCivicVariant();
            } else if (isMine) {
              const bx = x + w * 0.5,
                by = y + Math.round(roof * 0.3);
              rect(g, bx - 14, by - 3, 28, 12, 0x374451, 0.58);
              rect(g, bx - 11, by - 9, 7, 7, 0x60717e, 0.74);
              rect(g, bx + 3, by - 9, 7, 7, 0x60717e, 0.74);
              rect(g, bx - 10, by - 2, 20, 7, 0x83fff0, 0.18);
            }

            drawSignatureFacade();

            if (!['Great Mine', 'School'].includes(b.name)) {
              line(g, x + 5, wallStart + 6, x + w - 5, wallStart + 6, 0xffffff, 0.17, 2);
              line(g, x + 3, y + h - 12, x + w - 3, y + h - 12, 0x352c32, 0.34, 2);
              rect(g, x + 8, wallStart + 1, w - 16, 3, pal.trim, 0.12);
              rect(g, x + 8, y + h - 18, w - 16, 3, pal.accent, 0.08);
              for (let j = 0; j < b.w - 1; j++) {
                if (j % 2 !== 0) continue;
                rect(g, x + 12 + j * 32, y + h - 23, 12, 1, 0xffffff, 0.13);
              }
              if (isShop) {
                rect(g, x + 10, wallStart + 8, w - 20, 6, pal.accent, 0.14);
                rect(g, x + 14, wallStart + 13, w - 28, 2, 0xffffff, 0.14);
              }
            }

            const door = b.door * 32 + 16;
            if ((isShop || isCivic) && b.w >= 5 && !/mine/i.test(b.name)) { drawLantern(door - 24, y + h - 34); drawLantern(door + 24, y + h - 34); }
            rect(g, door - 17, y + h - 38, 34, 3, gold, 0.56);
            rect(g, door - 15, y + h - 31, 3, 29, 0xffefc2, 0.34);
            rect(g, door + 12, y + h - 31, 3, 29, 0xffefc2, 0.34);
            oval(g, door, y + h + 7, 54, 18, mineral ? 0x8be9e8 : 0xffd582, 0.1);

            if (b.w >= 5 && !/mine/i.test(b.name) && !isCivic) {
              for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
                let px = door + side * Math.min(48, w * 0.34),
                  py = y + h - 7;
                if (px < x + 22 || px > x + w - 22) continue;
                rect(g, px - 7, py - 8, 14, 10, 0x8f5b40, 0.92);
                rect(g, px - 9, py - 11, 18, 4, 0xe3bb7d, 0.8);
                for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++)
                  oval(
                    g,
                    px + 5 * k,
                    py - 17 - ((k + 3) % 2) * 4,
                    9,
                    10,
                    mineral ? 0x6bdbc9 : pal.leaf,
                    0.72,
                  );
              }
            }

            if (mineral) {
              for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
                let ox = x + (w * (j + 1)) / 3,
                  oy = y + roof * 0.7;
                g.fillStyle(0x78ffeb, 0.28).fillTriangle(ox, oy - 8, ox + 6, oy, ox, oy + 8);
                g.fillStyle(0x9cfafa, 0.48).fillTriangle(ox, oy - 6, ox - 5, oy, ox, oy + 6);
              }
            } else if (b.name === 'Town Hall' || b.name === 'Bank') {
              for (let xx = x + 13; xx < x + w - 13; xx += 32)
                rect(g, xx, wallStart + 13, 4, 13, 0xffedc5, 0.26);
            }
            window.LQWorldV15?.facade(g, b, a);
          } // Rendered after furniture into the cached indoor texture.
          function drawInterior(g, s) {
            const W = s.w * 32,
              H = s.h * 32;
            const isMine =
              s.floor === 'stone' ||
              s.floor === 'mine' ||
              s.items.some((i) => ['orepile', 'crystal', 'forge', 'minecart'].includes(i.k));
            // Wall architecture and baseboards that enhance depth without changing obstacles.
            for (let x = 9; x < W - 9; x += 32) {
              rect(g, x, 54, 24, 2, 0xfff0d2, 0.17);
              rect(g, x + 3, 58, 13, 1, 0x352a38, 0.18);
            }
            // Decorative wall lamps, translucent shafts, artistic floor reflections.
            for (let x = 45; x < W - 40; x += 112) {
              if (s.items.some((i) => i.y < 2 && i.x * 32 < x + 12 && i.x * 32 + i.w * 32 > x - 12))
                continue;
              oval(g, x, 46, 26, 14, 0xffe5ad, 0.21);
              rect(g, x - 5, 44, 10, 4, 0x9b7c51, 0.68);
              rect(g, x - 2, 40, 4, 6, 0xffeebc, 0.89);
              g.fillStyle(0xffdf97, 0.028).fillTriangle(
                x - 13,
                62,
                x + 13,
                62,
                x + 68,
                Math.min(H - 18, 158),
              );
              g.fillStyle(0xffe2a0, 0.026).fillTriangle(
                x - 13,
                62,
                x + 68,
                Math.min(H - 18, 158),
                x - 68,
                Math.min(H - 18, 158),
              );
            }
            // Carpet stitches, marble highlights, scattered small artistic floor mosaics.
            for (let row = 3; row < s.h - 1; row++)
              for (let cx = 1; cx < s.w - 1; cx++) {
                if (hash(cx, row, s.w) < 0.79) continue;
                let x = cx * 32 + hash(cx, row, 9) * 18 + 4,
                  y = row * 32 + hash(cx, row, 7) * 18 + 4;
                rect(g, x, y, 2, 1, isMine ? 0x96e9e0 : 0xfff6dd, 0.11);
              }
            for (const it of s.items) {
              const x = it.x * 32,
                y = it.y * 32,
                w = it.w * 32,
                h = it.h * 32,
                cx = x + w / 2;
              if (it.k === 'table' || it.k === 'desk' || it.k === 'counter') {
                if (w >= 60) {
                  // Individually shaded mugs and paperwork; no change to physical collision.
                  rect(g, x + 11, y + 8, 14, 10, 0xf8e6c5, 0.79);
                  line(g, x + 13, y + 11, x + 21, y + 11, 0x7e90a2, 0.59);
                  oval(g, x + w - 20, y + 10, 12, 9, 0xffedce, 0.87);
                  oval(g, x + w - 20, y + 9, 7, 5, 0x765142, 0.85);
                  rect(g, x + 3, y + 2, w - 6, 2, 0xffffff, 0.2);
                }
              } else if (it.k === 'sofa') {
                for (let k = 0; k < it.w; k++) {
                  const tx = x + k * 32 + 9;
                  rect(g, tx, y + 11, 15, 12, 0xffefd8, 0.19);
                  rect(g, tx + 3, y + 12, 3, 2, 0xffeacc, 0.34);
                }
              } else if (it.k === 'bed' || it.k === 'hospbed') {
                rect(g, x + 8, y + 12, 12, 4, 0xffffff, 0.42);
                rect(g, x + w - 15, y + h - 20, 9, 8, 0xffffff, 0.18);
              } else if (it.k === 'shelf' || it.k === 'goods') {
                for (let k = 0; k < Math.max(2, Math.floor(w / 18)); k++) {
                  rect(
                    g,
                    x + 6 + k * 16,
                    y + 6,
                    4,
                    10,
                    [0xffdf87, 0x75c4ae, 0xffb0a3, 0x7db2df][k % 4],
                    0.62,
                  );
                }
              } else if (it.k === 'plant') {
                oval(g, cx - 6, y + 10, 12, 7, 0xd8ffae, 0.45);
              } else if (
                it.k === 'crystal' ||
                it.k === 'forge' ||
                it.k === 'fireplace' ||
                it.k === 'lantern'
              ) {
                const warm = ['forge', 'fireplace', 'lantern'].includes(it.k),
                  color = warm ? 0xffb66f : 0x7bffee;
                for (let r = 2; r >= 1; r--) oval(g, cx, y + h / 2, 60 * r, 45 * r, color, 0.026);
                for (let j = 0; j < 5; j++)
                  rect(g, cx - 20 + j * 10, y + 7 + (j % 2) * 5, 2, 3, color, 0.5);
              } else if (it.k === 'stairs') {
                for (let sy = y + 6; sy < y + h - 6; sy += 9) {
                  rect(g, x + 5, sy, w - 10, 2, 0xffffff, 0.22);
                  rect(g, x + 8, sy + 2, w - 16, 1, 0x111b2e, 0.35);
                }
              }
            }
            // Architectural trim and vignette: corners are richer, central walk area stays clear.
            rect(g, 6, H - 6, W - 12, 3, 0xffe9c4, 0.12);
            rect(g, 7, 67, 3, H - 74, 0x16202b, 0.23);
            rect(g, W - 10, 67, 3, H - 74, 0x16202b, 0.23);
            for (const x of [18, W - 24])
              for (const y of [81, H - 25])
                oval(g, x, y, 14, 14, isMine ? 0x7bf2e4 : 0xffd4a1, 0.05);
            window.LQWorldV15?.interior(g, s);
            if (window.LQArtV69?.paintInterior?.(g, s)) {}
            else if (window.LQArtV68?.paintInterior) window.LQArtV68.paintInterior(g, s);
            else window.LQArtV67?.paintInterior(g, s);
            window.LQArtV74?.paintInterior?.(g, s);
          }
          const lightColor = (scene) => (mine(scene.area) ? 0x8df6ff : 0xffe8ad);
          const destroy = (scene) => {
            const arr = scene.__lq9Visuals || [];
            for (const v of arr)
              try {
                v.node.destroy();
              } catch {}
            scene.__lq9Visuals = [];
          };
          const quality = () => {
            try {
              let x = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('lifequest_v8_preferences') || '{}');
              return x.performance === 'low' ? 0 : x.performance === 'high' ? 2 : 1;
            } catch {
              return 1;
            }
          };
          function createLights(scene) {
            destroy(scene);
            if (quality() === 0) return;
            const inside = scene.inside;
            const spots = [];
            if (inside) {
              const unit = 32 * inside.k;
              for (const it of inside.spec.items || []) {
                if (
                  ![
                    'forge',
                    'fireplace',
                    'lantern',
                    'crystal',
                    'stove',
                    'cakecase',
                    'screen',
                    'portal',
                  ].includes(it.k)
                )
                  continue;
                if (spots.length >= 13) break;
                spots.push({
                  x: Yr + (it.x + it.w / 2) * unit,
                  y: Xr + (it.y + it.h / 2) * unit,
                  color: ['forge', 'fireplace', 'stove', 'cakecase'].includes(it.k)
                    ? 0xffad54
                    : 0x7feddc,
                  interior: true,
                });
              }
              for (let i = 1; i < inside.spec.w - 1; i += 4) {
                if (spots.length >= 16) break;
                spots.push({
                  x: Yr + (i + 0.5) * unit,
                  y: Xr + 1.7 * unit,
                  color: 0xffd08c,
                  interior: true,
                  window: true,
                });
              }
            } else {
              const max = scene.area.theme === 'art' ? (quality() === 2 ? 20 : 12) : (quality() === 2 ? 17 : 10);
              const buildings = [...scene.area.buildings].sort((a, b) => b.w * b.h - a.w * a.h);
              for (const b of buildings) {
                if (spots.length >= max) break;
                spots.push({
                  x: b.door * 32 + 16,
                  y: (b.y + b.h) * 32 - 25,
                  color: scene.area.theme === 'art' ? [0xff4f87,0x35c8d0,0xffd447,0x9470e8][spots.length % 4] : lightColor(scene),
                  interior: false,
                });
              }
              for (const o of scene.area.obstacles) {
                if (o.kind !== 'lamp' || spots.length >= max + 7) continue;
                spots.push({
                  x: (o.x + 0.5) * 32,
                  y: (o.y + 0.3) * 32,
                  color: 0xffed9e,
                  interior: false,
                });
              }
            }
            const glows = [];
            for (const s of spots) {
              // Soft light pools are rendered above the night tint, but behind UI.
              const radius = s.window ? 40 : s.interior ? 58 : 42;
              const a = scene.add
                .circle(s.x, s.y, radius, s.color, 0.055)
                .setDepth(s.interior ? 4 : 51)
                .setBlendMode(Y.BlendModes.ADD);
              const b = scene.add
                .circle(s.x, s.y, Math.max(9, radius * 0.35), s.color, 0.095)
                .setDepth(s.interior ? 4 : 51)
                .setBlendMode(Y.BlendModes.ADD);
              scene.__lq9Visuals.push({ node: a }, { node: b });
              glows.push({
                a,
                b,
                x: s.x,
                y: s.y,
                phase: hash(s.x | 0, s.y | 0) * 6.28,
                color: s.color,
                interior: s.interior,
              });
            }
            // A tiny pool of luminous fireflies (English Town) or floating ore motes (Minerals Town).
            const particles = [];
            const total = quality() === 2 ? 22 : 9;
            const rocky = mine(scene.area);
            const artistic = scene.area.theme === 'art';
            if (!inside) {
              const candidates = [];
              for (let i = 0; i < total * 8; i++) {
                const x = 32 + hash(i, 31) * Math.max(64, scene.area.w * 32 - 64);
                const y = 32 + hash(i, 72) * Math.max(64, scene.area.h * 32 - 64);
                const tx = (x / 32) | 0,
                  ty = (y / 32) | 0;
                if (tileRoad(scene.area, tx, ty) || avoid(scene.area, tx, ty)) continue;
                candidates.push({ x, y });
                if (candidates.length >= total) break;
              }
              for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
                const base = candidates[i];
                const dot = scene.add
                  .circle(base.x, base.y, rocky ? 2.8 : 2, artistic ? [0xff4f87,0x35c8d0,0xffd447,0x9470e8,0x83c96b][i % 5] : rocky ? 0x85fff0 : 0xfff0a8, artistic ? 0.38 : 0.3)
                  .setDepth(51)
                  .setBlendMode(Y.BlendModes.ADD);
                scene.__lq9Visuals.push({ node: dot });
                particles.push({
                  node: dot,
                  x: base.x,
                  y: base.y,
                  p: i * 0.63 + hash(i, 3) * 3,
                  rocky,
                  art: artistic,
                });
              }
            } else if (
              inside.spec.items.some((item) => item.k === 'crystal' || item.k === 'forge')
            ) {
              for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(total, 12); i++) {
                const anchor = inside.spec.items.filter(
                  (item) => item.k === 'crystal' || item.k === 'forge',
                )[
                  i %
                    inside.spec.items.filter((item) => item.k === 'crystal' || item.k === 'forge')
                      .length
                ];
                const unit = 32 * inside.k,
                  baseX = Yr + (anchor.x + 0.5) * unit,
                  baseY = Xr + (anchor.y + 0.5) * unit;
                const dot = scene.add
                  .circle(baseX, baseY, 2.8, anchor.k === 'forge' ? 0xffb072 : 0x85fff0, 0.24)
                  .setDepth(8)
                  .setBlendMode(Y.BlendModes.ADD);
                scene.__lq9Visuals.push({ node: dot });
                particles.push({
                  node: dot,
                  x: baseX,
                  y: baseY,
                  p: i * 0.88 + hash(i, 5) * 4,
                  rocky: true,
                  interior: true,
                });
              }
            }
            scene.__lq9Particles = particles;
            scene.__lq9Glows = glows;
            scene.__lq9Tick = 0;
          }
          function tick(scene, delta) {
            if (!scene || !scene.__lq9Glows || scene.switching) return;
            const q = quality();
            if (q === 0) {
              for (const g of scene.__lq9Glows) {
                g.a.setAlpha(0);
                g.b.setAlpha(0);
              }
              return;
            }
            scene.__lq9Tick = (scene.__lq9Tick || 0) + delta;
            if (scene.__lq9Tick < 90) return;
            scene.__lq9Tick = 0;
            const t = performance.now() / 1000;
            const minute = scene.store?.game?.minutes || 720;
            const dark = minute < 360 || minute > 1080;
            for (const g of scene.__lq9Glows) {
              const fade = g.interior ? 0.6 : dark ? 1 : 0.08;
              const wave = 0.87 + 0.13 * Math.sin(t * (g.color === 0xffad54 ? 5.5 : 2) + g.phase);
              g.a.setAlpha(fade * wave * (q === 2 ? 1 : 0.65));
              g.b.setAlpha(fade * (0.87 + 0.13 * Math.cos(t * 3 + g.phase)) * (q === 2 ? 1 : 0.65));
            }
            for (const p of scene.__lq9Particles || []) {
              const alive = p.interior || p.rocky || p.art || dark;
              p.node.setVisible(alive);
              if (!alive) continue;
              const sp = t * 0.65 + p.p;
              p.node.setPosition(p.x + Math.sin(sp) * 10, p.y + Math.cos(sp * 1.3) * 8);
              p.node.setAlpha((0.28 + 0.23 * Math.sin(sp * 2)) * (q === 2 ? 1 : 0.7));
            }
          }

          function v11ArtTerrain(g, a) {
            const W=a.w,H=a.h,T=32;
            const palette=[0xff4f87,0x35c8d0,0xffd447,0x9470e8,0x83c96b,0xff893d];
            for(let y=1;y<H-1;y++) for(let x=1;x<W-1;x++) {
              if(avoid(a,x,y)) continue;
              const p=hash(x,y,505), q=hash(x,y,606), px=x*T, py=y*T;
              if(tileRoad(a,x,y)) {
                if(p>0.70) line(g,px+5,py+22,px+13,py+20,0x20262d,0.35,1);
                if(p<0.10) {
                  oval(g,px+18,py+16,19,8,0x1e252d,0.3);
                  line(g,px+11,py+15,px+21,py+15,0xaeb9c1,0.2,1);
                }
                if(q>0.88) rect(g,px+5,py+6,3,2,palette[(q*palette.length)|0],0.50);
              } else {
                if(p>0.87) {
                  const c=palette[(q*palette.length)|0];
                  rect(g,px+6,py+7,6,2,c,0.66);
                  rect(g,px+11,py+9,2,5,c,0.58);
                  rect(g,px+17,py+13,4,2,c,0.52);
                }
                if(q>0.95) {
                  oval(g,px+18,py+22,6,3,palette[(p*palette.length)|0],0.72);
                  rect(g,px+17,py+20,3,1,0xffffff,0.5);
                }
              }
            }
            // Small stencil-like marks near the workshop and plaza, all decorative and non-blocking.
            for(const [tx,ty,c] of [[17,21,0xff4f87],[48,21,0x35c8d0],[18,38,0xffd447],[49,38,0x9470e8]]) {
              const x=tx*T,y=ty*T;
              line(g,x+3,y+15,x+13,y+4,c,0.78,3);
              line(g,x+13,y+4,x+23,y+15,c,0.78,3);
              line(g,x+23,y+15,x+3,y+15,c,0.78,2);
            }
            // Faded triple-mark clue at the old freight shed in Minerals Town.
            if(a.id==='mining-town') {
              const x=50*T,y=41*T;
              line(g,x+14,y+24,x+25,y+10,0xff4f87,0.58,3);
              line(g,x+25,y+10,x+36,y+24,0xff4f87,0.58,3);
              line(g,x+36,y+24,x+14,y+24,0xff4f87,0.58,3);
              g.lineStyle(3,0x35c8d0,0.52).strokeCircle(x+160,y+24,10);
              line(g,x+194,y+16,x+207,y+25,0x9470e8,0.56,3);
              line(g,x+207,y+25,x+194,y+34,0x9470e8,0.56,3);
            }
            // Tunnel floor details and warm safety lamps.
            if(a.id==='art-district') {
              const x=30*T,y=44*T,w=12*T;
              for(let yy=y+12;yy<y+12*T-8;yy+=32) {
                rect(g,x+6,yy,w-12,1,0x9aa1a7,0.22);
                rect(g,x+6,yy+2,w-12,1,0x050a10,0.32);
              }
              for(const xx of [31,40]) {
                rect(g,xx*T+11,y+18,10,4,0x3b4248,1);
                oval(g,xx*T+16,y+24,26,14,0xffd447,0.14);
                rect(g,xx*T+13,y+18,6,3,0xffd447,0.9);
              }
              // A few bright paint drops on the tunnel floor guide the way out.
              for(let i=0;i<7;i++) {
                const xx=34*T+hash(i,71)*4*T, yy=y+48+i*40;
                oval(g,xx,yy,6,4,palette[i%palette.length],0.72);
              }
            }
          }
          /* LIFEQUEST V11 - high-contrast silhouettes, material detail and readable pixel art.
   Rendered exclusively into existing cached textures, so geometry/collisions remain identical. */
          function v11Terrain(g, a) {
            if (a.theme === 'art') { v11ArtTerrain(g, a); return; }
            drawTerrain(g, a);
            const mining = mine(a);
            const W = a.w,
              H = a.h;
            for (let y = 1; y < H - 1; y++)
              for (let x = 1; x < W - 1; x++) {
                if (avoid(a, x, y)) continue;
                const p = hash(x, y, 111),
                  q = hash(x, y, 209),
                  px = x * 32,
                  py = y * 32;
                if (tileRoad(a, x, y)) {
                  // Fine aggregate, asphalt seams and restrained wear only where roads already exist.
                  if (p > 0.36) {
                    rect(
                      g,
                      px + 4 + ((q * 19) | 0),
                      py + 5 + ((p * 18) | 0),
                      3,
                      1,
                      mining ? 0xced6dc : 0xcbd1d8,
                      0.22,
                    );
                  }
                  if (p < 0.13) {
                    line(g, px + 9, py + 21, px + 15, py + 22, 0x151e2d, 0.3);
                    line(g, px + 15, py + 22, px + 19, py + 19, 0xffffff, 0.09);
                  }
                  if (p > 0.92) {
                    rect(g, px + 3, py + 3, 26, 1, 0xffffff, 0.07);
                    rect(g, px + 3, py + 4, 26, 1, 0x101825, 0.08);
                  }
                } else if (mining) {
                  // Hand-shaped angular shards and mineral streaks; visual-only.
                  if (p > 0.59) {
                    g.fillStyle(0x667d85, 0.28).fillTriangle(
                      px + 5,
                      py + 23,
                      px + 12,
                      py + 11,
                      px + 21,
                      py + 25,
                    );
                    line(g, px + 12, py + 11, px + 17, py + 20, 0xd4e9e0, 0.2);
                  }
                  if (q > 0.94) {
                    rect(g, px + 22, py + 7, 2, 6, 0x77dfd7, 0.48);
                    rect(g, px + 23, py + 6, 1, 2, 0xffffff, 0.53);
                  }
                } else {
                  if (p > 0.5) {
                    line(g, px + 8, py + 25, px + 10, py + 18, 0x28613e, 0.35);
                    line(g, px + 12, py + 25, px + 11, py + 20, 0xa8d480, 0.28);
                  }
                  if (q > 0.83) {
                    const c = [0xffd4a6, 0xf8e4a7, 0xb6dbff][((q * 100) | 0) % 3];
                    rect(g, px + 19, py + 14, 3, 3, c, 0.65);
                    rect(g, px + 20, py + 13, 1, 1, 0xffffff, 0.65);
                  }
                  if (p < 0.09) oval(g, px + 18, py + 22, 15, 6, 0x26502b, 0.1);
                }
              }
            // Kerbstones and real-world continuity. Only draw strips where an existing road meets an existing non-road tile.
            for (let y = 1; y < H - 1; y++)
              for (let x = 1; x < W - 1; x++) {
                if (!tileRoad(a, x, y) || avoid(a, x, y)) continue;
                const px = x * 32,
                  py = y * 32;
                if (!tileRoad(a, x, y - 1)) {
                  rect(g, px, py, 32, 3, mining ? 0x9f978d : 0xd8d4c7, 0.55);
                  rect(g, px, py + 3, 32, 2, 0x121921, 0.24);
                  for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++)
                    rect(g, px + k * 16 + 2, py + 1, 1, 2, 0xffffff, 0.14);
                }
                if (!tileRoad(a, x - 1, y)) {
                  rect(g, px, py, 3, 32, mining ? 0x9f978d : 0xd8d4c7, 0.52);
                  rect(g, px + 3, py, 2, 32, 0x121921, 0.2);
                }
              }
          }
          function v11Landscape(g, a) {
            drawLandscape(g, a);
            for (const o of a.obstacles) {
              const x = o.x * 32,
                y = o.y * 32,
                w = Math.max(1, o.w || 1) * 32,
                h = Math.max(1, o.h || 1) * 32;
              const id = hash(o.x, o.y, 678);
              if (o.kind === 'tree' || o.kind === 'pine') {
                // A leaf cluster with asymmetric facets rather than a circular glow.
                const cx = x + w / 2,
                  cy = y + Math.min(21, h * 0.38);
                const shade = o.kind === 'pine' ? 0x184d3f : 0x245b3b;
                oval(g, cx + 4, cy + 15, Math.min(w * 0.65, 35), 10, 0x0d2a25, 0.24);
                const colors =
                  o.kind === 'pine'
                    ? [0x376f61, 0x4e9877, 0x9ec9aa]
                    : [0x397d45, 0x69a65b, 0xb0d77d];
                for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
                  const xx = cx + [-11, 7, -3, 13, -8][k],
                    yy = cy + [-2, 3, -9, -5, 7][k];
                  oval(
                    g,
                    xx,
                    yy,
                    13 + (k % 2) * 5,
                    9 + (k % 3) * 2,
                    colors[k % 3],
                    k < 3 ? 0.31 : 0.2,
                  );
                }
                rect(g, cx - 2, cy - 7, 4, 2, 0xf6ffd8, 0.28);
                rect(g, cx + 8, cy + 1, 3, 2, 0xd8ffd0, 0.22);
              } else if (o.kind === 'rock' || o.kind === 'ore' || o.kind === 'boulder') {
                const c = mine(a) ? 0x7ca6b0 : 0x9cafa0;
                g.fillStyle(0x0b1721, 0.2).fillEllipse(
                  x + w * 0.5,
                  y + h * 0.83,
                  Math.min(w * 0.85, 47),
                  10,
                );
                g.fillStyle(c, 0.25).fillTriangle(
                  x + w * 0.25,
                  y + h * 0.47,
                  x + w * 0.57,
                  y + h * 0.2,
                  x + w * 0.75,
                  y + h * 0.61,
                );
                line(g, x + w * 0.57, y + h * 0.2, x + w * 0.7, y + h * 0.52, 0xffffff, 0.25);
                if (mine(a) && id > 0.3) {
                  rect(g, x + w * 0.38, y + h * 0.38, 3, 6, 0x81f3df, 0.45);
                }
              } else if (o.kind === 'pond' || o.kind === 'fountain' || o.kind === 'water') {
                for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                  const yy = y + 8 + k * Math.max(8, h / 5);
                  const sx = x + 9 + (k % 2) * 10;
                  line(g, sx, yy, sx + Math.min(w - 24, 25), yy, 0xdcffff, 0.25, 2);
                  rect(g, sx + 3, yy + 2, 12, 1, 0x1e658f, 0.27);
                }
              } else if (o.kind === 'lamp') {
                rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 3, y + 5, 6, 5, 0x493f39, 0.75);
                rect(g, x + w * 0.5 - 2, y + 5, 4, 3, 0xffe9ac, 0.87);
                line(g, x + w * 0.5 - 5, y + 11, x + w * 0.5 + 5, y + 11, 0xffd58c, 0.29, 2);
              }
            }
          }
          function v11Building(g, b, a) {
            drawBuilding(g, b, a);
            const mining = mine(a);
            const x = b.x * 32,
              y = b.y * 32,
              w = b.w * 32,
              h = b.h * 32;
            const roof = Math.round(h * ((b.floors ?? 1) === 2 ? 0.4 : 0.55));
            const n = String(b.name || '');
            const iron = /Mine|Smithy|Tool Shop|Rail Station|Station|Gem Shop/i.test(n);
            const civic = /Town Hall|Bank|Library|School|Hospital/i.test(n);
            const cafe = /Cafe|Restaurant|Hotel|Inn|Market/i.test(n);
            const timber = /House|Home|Cabin|Apartments/i.test(n);
            const edge = iron ? 0x768995 : civic ? 0xf0d4a5 : cafe ? 0xf6cfac : 0xdfc39c;
            // Roof-edge volume: highlights facing northwest; lower bevel facing southeast.
            rect(g, x + 9, y + 8, Math.max(5, w - 18), 3, edge, 0.32);
            rect(g, x + 7, y + roof - 9, Math.max(5, w - 14), 4, 0x1c2430, 0.19);
            line(g, x + 10, y + roof - 12, x + w - 10, y + roof - 12, edge, 0.3, 2);
            for (let row = y + 17, k = 0; row < y + roof - 14; row += 15, k++) {
              for (let xx = x + 16 + (k % 2) * 10; xx < x + w - 14; xx += 24) {
                if (iron) {
                  rect(g, xx, row, 15, 1, edge, 0.24);
                  rect(g, xx + 2, row + 3, 1, 5, 0x0d1722, 0.15);
                } else {
                  g.lineStyle(1, edge, 0.2).lineBetween(xx, row, xx + 15, row + 2);
                  rect(g, xx + 1, row + 3, 5, 1, 0xffffff, 0.13);
                }
              }
            }
            // Distinct facade identity with no new geometry outside building bounds.
            if (iron) {
              for (let j = 0; j < Math.min(5, b.w - 2); j++) {
                const xx = x + 18 + j * 32;
                rect(g, xx, y + roof + 8, 7, Math.max(7, h - roof - 34), 0x2e3944, 0.25);
                rect(g, xx + 1, y + roof + 8, 2, Math.max(7, h - roof - 34), 0xadc7b6, 0.13);
              }
              rect(g, x + w * 0.23, y + roof - 7, w * 0.54, 3, 0xf3cf8a, 0.4);
              if (/Gem Shop/.test(n))
                for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
                  const xx = x + 22 + j * 22;
                  g.fillStyle(0x76fff1, 0.28).fillTriangle(
                    xx,
                    y + roof * 0.65 - 6,
                    xx + 4,
                    y + roof * 0.65,
                    xx,
                    y + roof * 0.65 + 6,
                  );
                }
            } else if (civic) {
              for (let xx = x + 17; xx < x + w - 17; xx += 32) {
                rect(g, xx, y + roof + 11, 5, Math.max(8, h - roof - 36), 0xffebc2, 0.25);
                rect(g, xx + 5, y + roof + 11, 2, Math.max(8, h - roof - 36), 0x263042, 0.15);
              }
              rect(g, x + w * 0.28, y + roof * 0.5, Math.min(w * 0.45, 105), 3, 0xffe5b5, 0.29);
            } else if (cafe) {
              rect(g, x + 10, y + roof + 9, Math.max(8, w - 20), 5, 0xffd2a3, 0.32);
              for (let xx = x + 12; xx < x + w - 16; xx += 15) {
                rect(g, xx, y + roof + 10, 7, 4, 0xffffff, 0.28);
              }
            } else if (timber) {
              for (let yy = y + roof + 8; yy < y + h - 12; yy += 12)
                for (let xx = x + 12 + (((yy / 12) | 0) % 2) * 10; xx < x + w - 9; xx += 33) {
                  rect(g, xx, yy, 19, 1, 0x392e31, 0.15);
                  rect(g, xx + 4, yy + 2, 8, 1, 0xfff0ca, 0.11);
                }
            }
            // A distinct physical entrance: never draw across the door itself.
            const dc = b.door * 32 + 16;
            rect(g, dc - 16, y + h - 4, 32, 3, 0x1b2634, 0.25);
            rect(g, dc - 9, y + h - 16, 3, 2, 0xffe6a5, 0.67);
            rect(g, dc + 6, y + h - 16, 3, 2, 0xffe6a5, 0.67);
          }
          function v11Interior(g, s) {
            drawInterior(g, s);
            const W = s.w * 32,
              H = s.h * 32;
            const stone = s.floor === 'stone' || s.floor === 'mine';
            // Architectural details and natural-light shafts in wall band.
            rect(g, 7, 65, W - 14, 4, 0x0a1a29, 0.18);
            rect(g, 8, 68, W - 16, 2, 0xffebcb, 0.12);
            for (let x = 24; x < W - 28; x += 64) {
              rect(g, x, 3, 1, 45, 0xffffff, 0.12);
              rect(g, x + 2, 5, 2, 39, 0x10243a, 0.15);
            }
            for (const obj of s.items || []) {
              const x = obj.x * 32,
                y = obj.y * 32,
                w = obj.w * 32,
                h = obj.h * 32;
              // Existing furniture only: visual details cannot create a new obstacle.
              if (['table', 'counter', 'desk', 'schooldesk'].includes(obj.k)) {
                rect(g, x + 5, y + 4, Math.max(2, w - 10), 2, 0xffffff, 0.16);
                if (w >= 62) {
                  rect(g, x + 8, y + 8, 18, 11, 0xf9ebd7, 0.23);
                  rect(g, x + 11, y + 11, 13, 1, 0x42516b, 0.33);
                }
                if (w >= 92) {
                  oval(g, x + w - 19, y + 12, 12, 8, 0xfdf7de, 0.4);
                  oval(g, x + w - 19, y + 12, 7, 5, 0x684b44, 0.4);
                }
              } else if (obj.k === 'chair') {
                rect(g, x + 8, y + 8, Math.max(4, w - 16), 2, 0xffffff, 0.18);
              } else if (obj.k === 'shelf' || obj.k === 'goods') {
                for (let j = 0; j < Math.floor((w - 14) / 16); j++) {
                  rect(
                    g,
                    x + 7 + j * 16,
                    y + 6,
                    3 + (j % 2) * 2,
                    12,
                    [0xf5ca88, 0x83b5db, 0xd7ac9f, 0x99c99f][j % 4],
                    0.42,
                  );
                }
              } else if (obj.k === 'bed' || obj.k === 'hospbed') {
                rect(g, x + 7, y + 8, Math.max(4, w - 14), 2, 0xffffff, 0.21);
                rect(g, x + 6, y + h - 8, Math.max(4, w - 12), 1, 0x111d2a, 0.24);
              } else if (obj.k === 'rug') {
                for (let yy = y + 13; yy < y + h - 12; yy += 14) {
                  rect(g, x + 9, yy, Math.max(1, w - 18), 1, 0xffffff, 0.07);
                }
              } else if (obj.k === 'crystal') {
                line(g, x + w * 0.5, y + 4, x + w * 0.6, y + Math.max(8, h - 6), 0xe5ffff, 0.44);
                rect(g, x + w * 0.5, y + 6, 2, 6, 0xffffff, 0.45);
              } else if (obj.k === 'forge' || obj.k === 'fireplace') {
                for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++)
                  rect(g, x + 8 + j * 10, y + 9 + (j % 2) * 5, 3, 7, 0xffd28e, 0.45);
              }
            }
            // Transparent wall-edge inset and restrained warm windows; keep center of room unoccluded.
            rect(g, 8, H - 6, W - 16, 2, 0xfff1cb, 0.12);
            rect(g, 5, 70, 2, H - 78, 0x101c28, 0.18);
            rect(g, W - 7, 70, 2, H - 78, 0x101c28, 0.18);
            if (stone) rect(g, 9, 72, W - 18, 1, 0x80e4d5, 0.12);
            // V14.2: authored visual style for the room's actual building identity.
            // All strokes are paint on the cached room texture. No walkability/collision changes.
            let roomName = s.__lqVisualName;
            if (!roomName) {
              roomName = Object.keys(_r).find((name) => _r[name] === s) ||
                (s.labels && s.labels[0] && s.labels[0].text) || 'Interior';
              s.__lqVisualName = roomName;
            }
            const rn = String(roomName).toLowerCase();
            const isHeritage = /oriaris|cottage|granary|brown family/i.test(rn);
            const isCreative = /gallery|art|mural|photo|canvas|backbeat|studio/i.test(rn);
            const isMedical = /hospital|first-aid|clinic/i.test(rn);
            const isEducation = /library|school/i.test(rn);
            const isIndustry = /mine|smithy|freight|tool|gem|rail|quarry/i.test(rn);
            const isHome = /home|house|apartment|cottage|hotel|inn/i.test(rn);
            const isFood = /cafe|bakery|market|restaurant|corner shop/i.test(rn);
            const isCivic = /hall|bank|post office|police|church|fire station/i.test(rn);
            const theme = isCreative ? 'creative' : isHeritage ? 'heritage' : isMedical ? 'medical' :
              isEducation ? 'education' : isIndustry ? 'industrial' : isFood ? 'food' :
              isCivic ? 'civic' : isHome ? 'home' : 'neutral';
            const accentMap = {
              creative: 0xef81bf, heritage: 0xe3c494, medical: 0x80d0d7,
              education: 0xc4b18a, industrial: 0x85b4bb, food: 0xf0c388,
              civic: 0xd8c9a4, home: 0xd0b295, neutral: 0xc1c9d2,
            };
            const accent=accentMap[theme];
            const dark=theme === 'industrial' ? 0x253943 : theme === 'creative' ? 0x423753 : 0x57463d;
            // Architectural baseboards and wall panel separation under original furniture.
            rect(g, 7, 63, W-14, 3, dark, .36);
            rect(g, 7, 68, W-14, 2, accent, .30);
            rect(g, 7, 73, W-14, 2, 0xffffff, .12);
            rect(g, 8, H-15, W-16, 3, dark, .24);
            rect(g, 8, H-12, W-16, 1, accent, .16);
            // Narrow architectural border shadows and skirting: no floor-space obstruction.
            for (const px of [13,W-16]) {
              rect(g, px, 80, 3, Math.max(0,H-98), dark, .14);
              rect(g, px+1, 82, 1, Math.max(0,H-100), 0xffffff, .11);
            }
            // Wall panels with tiny material-specific marks.
            for (let px=25;px<W-25;px+=64) {
              rect(g, px, 8, 37, 2, accent, .23);
              rect(g, px+2, 12, 1, 28, dark, .16);
              rect(g, px+33, 12, 1, 28, dark, .14);
              rect(g, px+4, 42, 29, 1, accent, .20);
              if (theme==='heritage' || theme==='home') {
                rect(g,px+2,48,32,2,0xa27e59,.20);
                line(g,px+4,15,px+27,38,0x76573d,.08,1);
              } else if (theme==='creative') {
                for(let i=0;i<3;i++) rect(g,px+6+i*9,17+(i%2)*7,6,11,
                  [0xfaa0c9,0x75decf,0xf5d37b][i],.28);
              } else if (theme==='industrial') {
                rect(g,px+5,34,23,2,0xb1c5c7,.24);
                for(let i=0;i<3;i++) rect(g,px+8+i*8,18,2,2,0xf2e1bd,.35);
              } else {
                rect(g,px+6,18,24,16,accent,.06);
              }
            }
            // Signature crest/institution panel above the furnishing band.
            const crestX=W/2, crestY=31;
            oval(g,crestX,crestY,46,36,accent,.08);
            if(theme==='medical') {
              rect(g,crestX-4,crestY-15,8,30,0xf8ffff,.75);
              rect(g,crestX-15,crestY-4,30,8,0xf8ffff,.75);
              rect(g,crestX-2,crestY-13,4,26,0xc75555,.89);
              rect(g,crestX-13,crestY-2,26,4,0xc75555,.89);
            } else if(theme==='education') {
              rect(g,crestX-18,crestY-9,16,19,0xd3b987,.74);
              rect(g,crestX+2,crestY-9,16,19,0xe3cda9,.78);
              line(g,crestX,crestY-8,crestX,crestY+10,0x584a3e,.8,2);
              line(g,crestX-17,crestY-10,crestX-1,crestY-6,0xffffff,.3,1);
              line(g,crestX+1,crestY-6,crestX+17,crestY-10,0xffffff,.3,1);
            } else if(theme==='creative') {
              for(let j=0;j<4;j++) {
                const px=crestX+(j-1.5)*10;
                rect(g,px,crestY-12+j%2*4,7,19,
                  [0xffbc66,0x81d9c8,0xf388b8,0x9f8de9][j],.78);
              }
              dot(g,0xffffff,crestX,crestY-15,2,.65);
            } else if(theme==='industrial') {
