              g.fillStyle(0x7fd7cf,.68).fillTriangle(crestX,crestY-17,crestX-12,crestY+9,crestX+11,crestY+9);
              g.fillStyle(0xdbfcf5,.54).fillTriangle(crestX,crestY-17,crestX-12,crestY+9,crestX-2,crestY+8);
            } else if(theme==='heritage') {
              rect(g,crestX-15,crestY-11,30,22,0x76523d,.81);
              g.fillStyle(0xd6b782,.81).fillTriangle(crestX-18,crestY-11,crestX+18,crestY-11,crestX,crestY-23);
              rect(g,crestX-3,crestY-1,6,11,0xf5d9a4,.45);
            } else if(theme==='food') {
              oval(g,crestX,crestY,31,21,0xffefd7,.77);
              oval(g,crestX,crestY,21,12,0x9b7350,.69);
              rect(g,crestX-11,crestY+11,22,3,accent,.85);
              dot(g,0xf9eee0,crestX-5,crestY-2,3,.66);
            } else if(theme==='civic') {
              oval(g,crestX,crestY,32,32,0x5d6679,.62);
              oval(g,crestX,crestY,25,25,accent,.44);
              rect(g,crestX-4,crestY-9,8,18,0xf8e2b8,.51);
              rect(g,crestX-12,crestY+5,24,4,0xeed7ae,.54);
            } else {
              oval(g,crestX,crestY,20,20,accent,.34);
            }
            // Floor craftsmanship only: micro highlights along tiles and carpet edges.
            // Do not draw large furniture-sized shapes in walkable tiles.
            if (theme==='creative') {
              for(let ty=3;ty<s.h-1;ty+=2) for(let tx=1;tx<s.w-1;tx+=2) {
                const hsh=hash(tx,ty,s.w*9+s.h);
                if(hsh<.73)continue;
                rect(g,tx*32+4+(hsh*9|0),ty*32+15,8,2,
                  [0xe8a9d4,0x90cfcc,0xeacc9b][(tx+ty)%3],.18);
              }
            } else if (s.floor==='wood') {
              for(let yy=102;yy<H-14;yy+=32) for(let xx=22;xx<W-20;xx+=88) {
                rect(g,xx+(yy%64),yy,18,1,0xffe6ba,.10);
                rect(g,xx+(yy%64)+20,yy+3,10,1,0x3b281e,.1);
              }
            } else if (s.floor==='tile'||s.floor==='checker'||s.floor==='stone') {
              for(let yy=96;yy<H-16;yy+=64) for(let xx=18;xx<W-16;xx+=64) {
                rect(g,xx,yy,7,1,accent,.11);
                rect(g,xx,yy,1,7,accent,.11);
              }
            }
            // Extra detail on EXISTING furniture only, preserving room furniture footprint.
            for(const obj of s.items||[]){
              const px=obj.x*32,py=obj.y*32,pw=obj.w*32,ph=obj.h*32;
              if(['shelf','goods','rack'].includes(obj.k)) {
                rect(g,px+4,py+3,Math.max(3,pw-8),2,accent,.28);
                for(let i=0;i<Math.min(9,Math.floor((pw-14)/11));i++)
                  rect(g,px+8+i*11,py+10+(i%2)*3,4,7,
                    [0xe3c68c,0xb5d2ce,0xa7c4ee,0xd6a9a0][i%4],.53);
              } else if(['desk','counter','table'].includes(obj.k)) {
                rect(g,px+4,py+2,Math.max(4,pw-8),2,0xffffff,.18);
                if(pw>60) {
                  rect(g,px+8,py+8,12,8,0xf4e8cf,.46);
                  line(g,px+10,py+11,px+18,py+11,0x6d8492,.41,1);
                }
              } else if(obj.k==='plant') {
                dot(g,0xd2f4af,px+16,py+8,3,.47);
                dot(g,0xf2e49e,px+11,py+14,2,.35);
              } else if(obj.k==='rug') {
                rect(g,px+4,py+4,Math.max(4,pw-8),2,accent,.16);
                rect(g,px+4,py+ph-6,Math.max(4,pw-8),2,accent,.16);
              }
            }
          }

          /* ===== MINERALS CENTRAL STATION: modern station south of Minerals Town (solid colours, no translucent overlays) ===== */
          function drawStation(g, a) {
            if (a.id !== 'mining-town' && a.id !== 'english-town') return;
            const T = 32;
            const K = {
              ink: 0x1b2630,
              steel: 0x2f3d48,
              steel2: 0x4a5a66,
              steel3: 0x7b8b96,
              con: 0xcfd4d3,
              con2: 0xc2c9c9,
              con3: 0xa9b2b4,
              seam: 0x97a1a4,
              white: 0xf3f6f4,
              teal: 0x16b3a5,
              teal2: 0x0f857d,
              glass: 0x8fdcd6,
              glass2: 0x62b9b8,
              glassDk: 0x2d6f78,
              amber: 0xf3b94d,
              red: 0xd9534f,
              green: 0x58c26a,
              roofA: 0x5b6a74,
              roofB: 0x4a5862,
              bal: 0x8a8478,
              bal2: 0x726c62,
              tie: 0x4a3a2c,
              tie2: 0x5f4a37,
              rail: 0xdde3e6,
              wood: 0xb98a52,
              wood2: 0x9c723f,
              soil: 0x5a3f2c,
            };
            const R = (x, y, w, h, c) => rect(g, x, y, w, h, c, 1);
            const pave = (x0, y0, x1, y1, c1, c2, sm) => {
              for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++)
                for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
                  const h = hash(x, y, 41);
                  R(x * T, y * T, T, T, (x + y) & 1 ? c1 : c2);
                  R(x * T, y * T + T - 1, T, 1, sm);
                  R(x * T + T - 1, y * T, 1, T, sm);
                  if (h > 0.88) R(x * T + 8 + (((h * 40) | 0) % 12), y * T + 10, 5, 1, sm);
                }
            };
            // Ground: forecourt, side walks, concourse, gateway, platforms.
            pave(4, 47, 55, 51, K.con, K.con2, K.seam);
            R(4 * T, 47 * T, 52 * T, 4, K.con3);
            pave(4, 52, 10, 62, K.con2, K.con, K.seam);
            pave(51, 52, 55, 62, K.con2, K.con, K.seam);
            pave(11, 58, 50, 62, 0xd8dcdb, 0xcdd2d1, K.seam);
            pave(28, 52, 32, 57, 0xb9c1c2, 0xafb8b9, K.seam);
            pave(4, 63, 55, 64, 0xbcc3c3, 0xb2baba, 0x9aa3a5);
            pave(4, 67, 55, 68, 0xbcc3c3, 0xb2baba, 0x9aa3a5);
            R(30 * T + 10, 52 * T, 12, 11 * T, K.teal);
            R(4 * T, 64 * T + T - 8, 52 * T, 6, K.amber);
            R(4 * T, 64 * T + T - 2, 52 * T, 2, K.ink);
            R(4 * T, 67 * T + 2, 52 * T, 6, K.amber);
            R(4 * T, 68 * T + T - 8, 52 * T, 6, K.amber);
            // Tracks.
            const track = (ty) => {
              const y = ty * T,
                W = a.w * T;
              R(0, y, W, 2 * T, K.bal);
              for (let xx = 0; xx < W; xx += 16)
                if (hash(xx >> 4, ty, 57) > 0.5) R(xx, y, 16, 2 * T, K.bal2);
              for (let xx = 4; xx < W; xx += 14) {
                R(xx, y + 6, 6, 2 * T - 12, K.tie);
                R(xx, y + 6, 2, 2 * T - 12, K.tie2);
              }
              R(0, y + 12, W, 4, K.rail);
              R(0, y + 16, W, 2, K.steel3);
              R(0, y + 44, W, 4, K.rail);
              R(0, y + 48, W, 2, K.steel3);
            };
            track(65);
            track(69);
            R(0, 71 * T, a.w * T, 8, K.bal2);
            // Hall: roof, glass curtain wall, sign band.
            const hall = (xa, xb) => {
              const x = xa * T,
                w = (xb - xa + 1) * T,
                ry = 52 * T,
                fy = 55 * T;
              R(x, ry, w, 3 * T, K.roofA);
              for (let xx = x + 16; xx < x + w; xx += 16) R(xx, ry + 4, 1, 3 * T - 12, K.roofB);
              for (let xx = x + 48; xx < x + w; xx += 96) R(xx, ry + 4, 2, 3 * T - 12, K.steel3);
              R(x + 10, ry + 14, w - 20, 34, K.steel);
              R(x + 13, ry + 17, w - 26, 28, K.glassDk);
              R(x + 13, ry + 17, w - 26, 14, K.glass2);
              for (let xx = x + 13; xx < x + w - 13; xx += 26) {
                R(xx, ry + 17, 2, 28, K.steel2);
                R(xx + 8, ry + 20, 3, 10, K.glass);
              }
              R(x, ry, w, 5, K.con3);
              R(x, ry + 3 * T - 26, w, 10, K.con);
              R(x, ry + 3 * T - 16, w, 3, K.ink);
              R(x, ry + 3 * T - 13, w, 5, K.con3);
              R(x, fy, w, 28, 0x24505a);
              R(x, fy, w, 3, K.teal);
              R(x, fy + 25, w, 3, K.ink);
              for (let xx = x + 8; xx < x + w - 8; xx += 64) {
                R(xx, fy + 10, 3, 3, K.amber);
                R(xx + 32, fy + 10, 3, 3, K.white);
              }
              R(x, fy + 28, w, 68, K.con3);
              R(x + 4, fy + 32, w - 8, 56, K.glass2);
              for (let xx = x + 4; xx < x + w - 4; xx += 32) R(xx, fy + 32, 2, 56, K.steel2);
              R(x + 4, fy + 56, w - 8, 2, K.steel2);
              for (let xx = x + 14; xx < x + w - 20; xx += 96) {
                R(xx, fy + 36, 5, 18, K.glass);
                R(xx + 9, fy + 40, 3, 10, K.glass);
                R(xx, fy + 60, 5, 22, K.glass);
              }
              R(x, fy + 90, w, 6, K.steel);
              R(x, 58 * T, w, 5, K.con3);
            };
            hall(11, 27);
            hall(33, 50);
            R(11 * T - 4, 52 * T, 4, 6 * T, K.steel);
            R(51 * T, 52 * T, 4, 6 * T, K.steel);
            // Sign band details: departure boards and train pictograms.
            const board = (tx) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = 55 * T + 4;
              R(x, y, 6 * T, 20, 0x10161c);
              R(x, y, 6 * T, 2, K.steel2);
              R(x + 4, y + 17, 6 * T - 8, 2, K.amber);
            };
            board(21);
            board(35);
            for (const tx of [12, 48]) {
              const x = tx * T + 4,
                y = 55 * T + 8;
              R(x, y, 24, 10, K.white);
              for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) R(x + 3 + k * 7, y + 2, 4, 4, K.glassDk);
              R(x + 2, y + 10, 4, 3, K.steel);
              R(x + 18, y + 10, 4, 3, K.steel);
            }
            // Gateway: steel frame, LED headers, columns.

            // Twin clock towers.
            R(28 * T - 6, 55 * T, 5 * T + 12, 10, K.steel);
            R(28 * T - 6, 55 * T + 10, 5 * T + 12, 3, K.teal);
            const tower = (tx) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = 47 * T;
              R(x, y + 34, 96, 5 * T - 34, K.con);
              R(x + 86, y + 34, 10, 5 * T - 34, K.con3);
              R(x, y + 34, 6, 5 * T - 34, K.white);
              R(x + 34, y + 58, 28, 84, K.steel);
              R(x + 37, y + 61, 22, 78, K.glassDk);
              R(x + 37, y + 61, 22, 36, K.glass2);
              R(x + 46, y + 61, 3, 78, K.steel2);
              for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                R(x + 12, y + 62 + k * 28, 12, 16, K.glassDk);
                R(x + 72, y + 62 + k * 28, 12, 16, K.glassDk);
              }
              R(x - 6, y, 108, 40, K.steel);
              R(x - 6, y, 108, 5, K.teal);
              oval(g, x + 48, y + 22, 34, 34, K.ink);
              oval(g, x + 48, y + 22, 30, 30, K.white);
              line(g, x + 48, y + 22, x + 48, y + 11, K.ink, 1, 2);
              line(g, x + 48, y + 22, x + 56, y + 26, K.ink, 1, 2);
              R(x - 10, y - 8, 116, 8, K.steel2);
              R(x + 44, y - 14, 8, 6, K.amber);
            };
            tower(25);
            tower(33);
            // Plaza dressing.
            const planter = (tx, ty) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T;
              R(x, y, 3 * T, 2 * T, K.con3);
              R(x + 4, y + 4, 3 * T - 8, 2 * T - 12, K.soil);
              R(x, y + 2 * T - 8, 3 * T, 8, K.steel2);
              for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
                const px = x + 10 + i * 12,
                  py = y + 14 + ((i * 7) % 3) * 8;
                oval(g, px, py, 14, 12, i % 2 ? 0x4aa85c : 0x6fcf7a);
                if (i % 3 === 0) R(px - 2, py - 8, 3, 3, [K.amber, K.red, 0xf2f2f2][(i / 3) | 0]);
              }
            };
            planter(14, 48);
            planter(44, 48);
            const lamp = (tx, ty) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T;
              R(x + 10, y + 26, 12, 5, K.steel2);
              R(x + 14, y + 6, 4, 22, K.steel);
              R(x + 8, y + 2, 16, 7, K.steel2);
              R(x + 10, y + 4, 12, 3, K.amber);
            };
            lamp(20, 49);
            lamp(40, 49);
            // Concourse fixtures.
            const counter = (tx, ty, tw) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T,
                w = tw * T;
              R(x, y, w, 13, 0xe9edea);
              R(x, y + 13, w, 19, K.steel2);
              R(x, y + 16, w, 3, K.teal);
              R(x, y + 29, w, 3, K.ink);
              for (let xx = x + 10; xx < x + w - 24; xx += 48) {
                R(xx, y + 2, 20, 8, K.ink);
                R(xx + 2, y + 4, 16, 4, K.green);
              }
              R(x + 2, y - 4, w - 4, 5, K.glass);
            };
            counter(15, 59, 6);
            counter(41, 59, 6);
            const bench = (tx, ty, tw) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T,
                w = tw * T;
              R(x, y + 4, w, 7, K.wood2);
              R(x, y + 12, w, 9, K.wood);
              for (let xx = x + 16; xx < x + w; xx += 16) R(xx, y + 12, 1, 9, K.wood2);
              R(x + 4, y + 21, 5, 9, K.steel);
              R(x + w - 9, y + 21, 5, 9, K.steel);
              R(x, y + 28, w, 3, K.con3);
            };
            bench(23, 60, 4);
            bench(34, 60, 4);
            const kiosk = (tx, ty, tc) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T,
                w = 3 * T;
              R(x, y + 14, w, 50, 0xeceeee);
              for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) R(x + i * 16, y, 16, 16, i % 2 ? K.white : tc);
              R(x, y + 16, w, 3, K.ink);
              R(x + 8, y + 24, w - 16, 22, K.glassDk);
              R(x + 8, y + 24, w - 16, 9, K.glass2);
              R(x + 4, y + 46, w - 8, 6, K.wood);
              R(x + 14, y + 40, 6, 6, K.amber);
              R(x + 60, y + 40, 6, 6, K.red);
              R(x, y + 56, w, 8, K.steel2);
            };
            kiosk(11, 60, K.teal);
            kiosk(48, 60, K.amber);
            const column = (tx, ty) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T;
              R(x + 10, y + 4, 12, 26, K.steel);
              R(x + 13, y + 4, 3, 26, K.steel3);
              R(x + 5, y, 22, 6, K.steel2);
              R(x + 6, y + 26, 20, 6, K.con3);
            };
            column(21, 62);
            column(40, 62);
            const fare = (tx, ty) => {
              const x = tx * T,
                y = ty * T;
              R(x + 7, y + 8, 18, 22, K.steel2);
              R(x + 7, y + 8, 18, 5, K.white);
              R(x + 13, y + 16, 6, 3, K.green);
              R(x + 9, y + 26, 14, 4, K.ink);
            };
            for (let tx = 22; tx <= 27; tx++) fare(tx, 62);
            for (let tx = 34; tx <= 39; tx++) fare(tx, 62);
            // Passenger train (Platform 1 side): white EMU with teal stripe.
            {
              const x = 15 * T,
                y = 65 * T,
                w = 32 * T,
                car = w / 4;
              R(x, y + 2, w, 16, 0xdde3e2);
              for (let xx = x + 20; xx < x + w - 20; xx += 64) {
                R(xx, y + 6, 22, 8, K.steel3);
                R(xx + 2, y + 8, 18, 2, K.con);
              }
              R(x, y + 18, w, 40, K.white);
              R(x, y + 24, w, 14, K.glassDk);
              R(x, y + 24, w, 3, K.glass2);
              for (let xx = x + 10; xx < x + w; xx += 20) R(xx, y + 24, 3, 14, K.white);
              R(x, y + 42, w, 6, K.teal);
              R(x, y + 58, w, 6, K.steel2);
              for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++)
                for (const d of [28, car / 2 - 10, car - 48]) {
                  const dx = x + car * c + d;
                  R(dx, y + 20, 22, 38, 0xc9d3d6);
                  R(dx + 3, y + 24, 16, 14, K.glassDk);
                  R(dx + 10, y + 20, 2, 38, K.steel2);
                }
              for (let c = 1; c < 4; c++) R(x + car * c - 3, y + 2, 6, 60, K.ink);
              oval(g, x + 4, y + 34, 36, 62, K.white);
              oval(g, x + w - 4, y + 34, 36, 62, K.white);
              R(x - 8, y + 24, 12, 14, K.ink);
              R(x + w - 4, y + 24, 12, 14, K.ink);
              R(x - 6, y + 42, 5, 5, K.amber);
              R(x + w + 1, y + 42, 5, 5, K.amber);
            }
            // Ore express (Platform 2 side): teal locomotive + five hopper wagons loaded with ore.
            {
              const x0 = 22 * T,
                y = 69 * T;
              R(x0, y + 2, 160, 14, 0x3b4a55);
              oval(g, x0 + 40, y + 9, 22, 10, K.steel);
              oval(g, x0 + 90, y + 9, 22, 10, K.steel);
              oval(g, x0 + 135, y + 9, 16, 8, K.steel);
              R(x0, y + 16, 160, 40, 0x1b8f8a);
              R(x0, y + 16, 160, 5, 0x23b3ab);
              R(x0, y + 44, 160, 6, K.amber);
              R(x0 + 10, y + 22, 30, 14, K.ink);
              R(x0 + 13, y + 25, 24, 5, K.glass2);
              for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                R(x0 + 60 + k * 22, y + 22, 12, 16, 0x146f6b);
                R(x0 + 60 + k * 22, y + 22, 2, 16, 0x23b3ab);
              }
              R(x0, y + 56, 160, 8, K.ink);
              R(x0 + 2, y + 34, 4, 6, K.amber);
              for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                const wx = x0 + 160 + i * 160;
                R(wx + 4, y + 4, 152, 58, 0x6b4a36);
                R(wx + 8, y + 7, 144, 22, 0x2a3035);
                for (let k = 0; k < 11; k++) {
                  const px = wx + 14 + k * 13,
                    py = y + 12 + (k % 3) * 3;
                  oval(g, px, py, 16, 10, [0x5d6a72, 0x7b8f95, 0x2e7d7a, 0x8a6a3a][k % 4]);
                  R(px - 2, py - 3, 3, 2, 0xdfe7e6);
                }
                R(wx + 4, y + 30, 152, 26, 0x7a5640);
                R(wx + 4, y + 30, 152, 3, 0x8e6850);
                for (let xx = wx + 20; xx < wx + 150; xx += 28) R(xx, y + 33, 2, 22, 0x5c3f2d);
                R(wx + 10, y + 56, 140, 6, K.ink);
                R(wx, y + 30, 4, 6, K.ink);
              }
            }
          }
          function stationLabels(s) {
            if (s.area.id !== 'mining-town' && s.area.id !== 'english-town') return;
            const T = 32,
              mk = (txt, tx, py, color, bg) =>
                s.areaObjs.push(
                  s.add
                    .text(tx * T, py, txt, {
                      fontSize: '12px',
                      color,
                      backgroundColor: bg,
                      padding: { x: 4, y: 2 },
                    })
                    .setOrigin(0.5)
                    .setDepth(12),
                );
            mk(
              s.area.id === 'mining-town'
                ? 'MINERALS CENTRAL STATION'
                : 'ENGLISH TOWN CENTRAL STATION',
              30.5,
              51 * T + 10,
              '#ffffff',
              '#1b2630',
            );
            mk('TICKETS', 17.5, 55 * T + 14, '#ffffff', '#16b3a5');
            mk('CARGO & PARCELS', 44, 55 * T + 14, '#1b2630', '#f3b94d');
            mk('DEPARTURES', 24, 55 * T + 14, '#f3b94d', '#10161c');
            mk('DEPARTURES', 38, 55 * T + 14, '#f3b94d', '#10161c');
            mk('CAFE', 12.5, 60 * T - 8, '#ffffff', '#0f857d');
            mk('NEWS & SNACKS', 49.5, 60 * T - 8, '#1b2630', '#f3b94d');
            mk('PLATFORM 1', 8, 63 * T + 10, '#ffffff', '#1b2630');
            mk('PLATFORM 2', 8, 67 * T + 10, '#ffffff', '#1b2630');
            const top = (key, x0, y0, w, h, draw) => {
              if (!s.textures.exists(key)) {
                const g = s.add.graphics(),
                  L = (x, y, ww, hh, c) => rect(g, x, y, ww, hh, c, 1);
                draw(L);
                g.generateTexture(key, w, h);
                g.destroy();
              }
              s.areaObjs.push(s.add.image(x0, y0, key).setOrigin(0).setDepth(10.6));
            };
            top('lq-st-gate', 28 * T - 6, 52 * T, 5 * T + 12, 6 * T, (L) => {
              L(6, 0, 5, 6 * T, 0x2f3d48);
              L(5 * T + 1, 0, 5, 6 * T, 0x2f3d48);
              L(0, 0, 5 * T + 12, 12, 0x2f3d48);
              L(0, 12, 5 * T + 12, 4, 0x16b3a5);
            });
            const pillar = (L) => {
              L(10, 4, 12, 26, 0x2f3d48);
              L(13, 4, 3, 26, 0x7b8b96);
              L(5, 0, 22, 6, 0x4a5a66);
              L(6, 26, 20, 6, 0xa9b2b4);
            };
            top('lq-st-pillar', 13 * T, 62 * T, T, T, pillar);
            s.areaObjs.push(
              s.add
                .image(48 * T, 62 * T, 'lq-st-pillar')
                .setOrigin(0)
                .setDepth(10.6),
            );
          }
          return {
            drawTerrain: v11Terrain,
            drawLandscape: v11Landscape,
            drawBuilding: v11Building,
            drawInterior: v11Interior,
            drawStation,
            stationLabels,
            createLights,
            destroy,
            tick,
          };
        })();

          function classify(area, name) {
            const s=String(name||'').toLowerCase();
            if(area==='vocalt-greedy-land') {
              if(/metal|forge|copper|smith/.test(s))return 'metal';
              if(/jewel|sapphire|gem/.test(s))return 'jewels';
              if(/chronos|clock/.test(s))return 'clockmaker';
              if(/ribbon|reed|cloth|fabric|weav/.test(s))return 'tailor';
              if(/pottery|terracotta/.test(s))return 'pottery';
              if(/book|map|ledger/.test(s))return 'archive';
              if(/grand exchange|coin/.test(s))return 'exchange';
              if(/vocalt|spire/.test(s))return 'lord';
              if(/kitchen|tavern|spice/.test(s))return 'food';
              if(/caravan|depot/.test(s))return 'warehouse';
              return 'courtyard';
            }
            if(area==='old-oriaris') {
              if(/opera/.test(s))return 'opera';
              if(/academy/.test(s))return 'academy';
              if(/dynast|gallery/.test(s))return 'gallery';
              if(/aurelia/.test(s))return 'roses';
              if(/valmont/.test(s))return 'archive';
              if(/seraphine/.test(s))return 'conservatory';

          function drawFront(g,s){
            const d=s.__lq53;if(!d)return;
            const p=d.theme,role=d.role, W=s.w*32,H=s.h*32,cx=W/2;
            const R=(x,y,w,h,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(x,y,Math.max(0,w),Math.max(0,h));
            const L=(x,y,X,Y,c,a=.65,w=1)=>g.lineStyle(w,c,a).lineBetween(x,y,X,Y);
            const C=(x,y,r,c,a=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,a).fillCircle(x,y,r);
            // Soft integrated foreground craftsmanship: never covers real furniture or walkable floor.
            if(p.id==='living'){
              for(let xx=13;xx<W;xx+=66){L(xx,4,xx+14,20,0x204a35,.68,3);C(xx+14,19,3,0x9fca7c,.56);}
              for(let xx=10;xx<W-10;xx+=90){R(xx,57,45,2,0xc2d297,.24);}
            } else if(p.id==='royal') {
              for(let xx=27;xx<W-20;xx+=85){R(xx+9,54,36,5,0xedc886,.57);R(xx+25,54,4,12,0xf5dfb3,.43);}
            } else if(p.id==='industrial') {
              for(let xx=22;xx<W-20;xx+=80){R(xx,52,6,14,0xc0cdcb,.46);R(xx-7,60,20,4,0x202f38,.67);}
            } else if(p.id==='tech') {
              for(let xx=24;xx<W-20;xx+=76){R(xx,54,47,3,0x8bf6e5,.57);R(xx+12,59,22,2,0x135f78,.5);}
            } else if(p.id==='atelier') {
              for(let xx=14,i=0;xx<W-14;xx+=61,i++){R(xx,52,50,3,[0xf1cb8b,0xb9a7f1,0x8de4d0][i%3],.65);}
            } else if(p.id==='bazaar') {
              for(let xx=18;xx<W-18;xx+=64){R(xx,53,39,4,0xf3d4a1,.47);R(xx+5,58,29,2,p.ink,.45);}
              if(role==='lord'){R(cx-29,17,58,5,p.accent,.65);}
            } else {
              for(let xx=12;xx<W-14;xx+=66){R(xx,53,44,3,p.accent,.24);}
            }
          }
          const movable = new Set(['table','chair','sofa','counter','desk','schooldesk','shelf','goods','plant','crate','barrel','locker','pew','glass','lantern','rug','mat','paint','poster','clock','flag']);
          const wallDecor = new Set(['paint','poster','clock','flag','glass','mirror','pigeon','board','menu','screen','cross']);
          const passableDecor = new Set(['rug','mat',...wallDecor]);
          const keep = new Set(['stairs','portal','altar','cell','vault','firetruck','bed','hospbed','forge','fireplace','stove','minecart','orepile','crystal','partition','piano']);
          function reflow(spec,theme,seed) {
            // Reflow generic rooms. Preserve curated huge layouts, story-specific fixtures and all existing item kinds.
            const source=spec.__lq53Source;
            if(spec.w>23||spec.h>16||source.length<5)return {items:source.map(v=>({...v})),moved:0,curated:true};
            const w=spec.w,h=spec.h, cx=Math.floor(w/2), side=(theme==='royal'||theme==='tech')?2:1;
            const blocked=Array.from({length:h},()=>Array(w).fill(false));
            const reserved=Array.from({length:h},()=>Array(w).fill(false));
            const paintCells=(arr,obj,value)=>{for(let y=obj.y;y<obj.y+obj.h;y++)for(let x=obj.x;x<obj.x+obj.w;x++)if(x>=0&&y>=0&&x<w&&y<h)arr[y][x]=value;};
            for(let y=h-3;y<h;y++)for(let x=cx-1;x<=cx+1;x++)if(x>=0&&x<w)reserved[y][x]=true;
            for(const pos of [...Object.values(spec.spots||{}),...(spec.guests||[])]){
              if(!Array.isArray(pos))continue; const x=pos[0]|0,y=pos[1]|0;
              if(y>=2&&y<h&&x>=0&&x<w){reserved[y][x]=true;for(let xx=x-1;xx<=x+1;xx++)if(xx>=0&&xx<w)reserved[y][xx]=true;}
            }
            const fixed=[],floorItems=[],decor=[];
            for(const it of source){ const v={...it};if(wallDecor.has(v.k)){decor.push(v);continue;}
              if(keep.has(v.k)||!movable.has(v.k)){fixed.push(v);if(!passableDecor.has(v.k))paintCells(blocked,v,true);continue;}
              floorItems.push(v);
            }
            // Keep immovable story fixtures, including stair/portal and the bedroom's own bed.
            const result=[...fixed],placed=[];
            const canPlace=(obj,x,y)=>{
              if(x<1||y<2||x+obj.w>w-1||y+obj.h>=h-1)return false;
              for(let yy=y;yy<y+obj.h;yy++)for(let xx=x;xx<x+obj.w;xx++)if(blocked[yy][xx]||reserved[yy][xx])return false;
              return true;
            };
            const cells=[];
            // Each culture has a different spatial plan. Right/left bay priority, ring, axial or staggered benches.
            for(let y=2;y<h-2;y++)for(let x=1;x<w-1;x++){
              const dx=Math.abs(x+0.5-w/2),dy=Math.abs(y+0.5-h*.52);
              let rank=0;
              if(theme==='civic')rank=(x<w*.47?0:5)+y*2+(x%3);
              else if(theme==='industrial')rank=Math.abs(x-w*.22)*2+Math.min(y,h-3-y)*3;
              else if(theme==='heritage'||theme==='ranger')rank=Math.abs(Math.hypot(dx/w,dy/h)-.28)*42+(x%2)*2;
              else if(theme==='atelier')rank=Math.abs((x*3+y*2)%(Math.max(5,w-4)))*2+Math.abs(x-w*.3);
              else if(theme==='tech')rank=(y%3)*12+Math.abs(x-w*.25)*2;
              else if(theme==='royal')rank=dx<2?30:dx+Math.abs(y-h*.35)*.8;
              else if(theme==='living')rank=Math.abs(Math.hypot(dx/w,dy/h)-.31)*58;
              else if(theme==='bazaar')rank=(x<w*.34?0:x>w*.7?4:18)+y*.8;
              rank+=(hash(seed+'|'+x+'|'+y)%7)*.09;
              cells.push({x,y,rank});
            }
            cells.sort((a,b)=>a.rank-b.rank);
            // Large fixtures first: physically meaningful bays; chairs/plant accents are placed last.
            floorItems.sort((a,b)=>b.w*b.h-a.w*a.h||(hash(seed+a.k)-hash(seed+b.k)));
            for(const item of floorItems){
              if(passableDecor.has(item.k)) {placed.push({...item});continue;}
              const preferBack=/^(counter|desk|shelf|goods|locker|crate|barrel|schooldesk)$/.test(item.k);
              const options=preferBack?[...cells].sort((a,b)=>a.rank-b.rank+(a.y-b.y)*.7):cells;
              let target=null;
              for(const p of options){if(canPlace(item,p.x,p.y)){target={x:p.x,y:p.y};break;}}
              if(!target&&canPlace(item,item.x,item.y))target={x:item.x,y:item.y};
              if(!target)return {items:source.map(v=>({...v})),moved:0,curated:true,reason:'capacity'};
              const v={...item,...target};paintCells(blocked,v,true);placed.push(v);
            }
            // Every fixed interaction has to remain reachable from the door. Reject the reflow otherwise.
            const reach=new Set(),q=[];
            const enqueue=(x,y)=>{if(x<1||x>=w-1||y<2||y>=h-1||blocked[y][x])return;let k=x+','+y;if(reach.has(k))return;reach.add(k);q.push([x,y]);};
            enqueue(cx,h-2);if(!q.length)return {items:source.map(v=>({...v})),moved:0,curated:true,reason:'door'};
            for(let j=0;j<q.length;j++){const [x,y]=q[j];for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]])enqueue(x+dx,y+dy);}
            const accessible=(it)=>{
              if(passableDecor.has(it.k))return true;
              for(let y=it.y-1;y<=it.y+it.h;y++)for(let x=it.x-1;x<=it.x+it.w;x++){
                if(x>=it.x&&x<it.x+it.w&&y>=it.y&&y<it.y+it.h)continue;
                if(reach.has(x+','+y))return true;
              }
              return false;
            };
            if(![...fixed,...placed].every(accessible))return {items:source.map(v=>({...v})),moved:0,curated:true,reason:'navigation'};
            for(const pos of [...Object.values(spec.spots||{}),...(spec.guests||[])]){
              if(!Array.isArray(pos))continue;
              const key=(pos[0]|0)+','+(pos[1]|0);
              if(!reach.has(key))return {items:source.map(v=>({...v})),moved:0,curated:true,reason:'npc'};
            }
            const moved=placed.filter(it=>{const o=source.find(s=>s.k===it.k&&s.w===it.w&&s.h===it.h&&s.c===it.c);return o&&(o.x!==it.x||o.y!==it.y)}).length;
            return {items:[...result,...placed,...decor],moved,curated:false};
          }
          const counts={prepared:0,reflowed:0,kept:0,byTheme:{}};
          function prepare(area,building,spec,floor=0){
            if(!spec||!Array.isArray(spec.items))return null;
            const theme=THEMES[area]||THEMES['english-town'];
            const key=area+'|'+building+'|'+floor;
            if(spec.__lq53?.key===key)return spec.__lq53;
            if(!spec.__lq53Source)spec.__lq53Source=spec.items.map(i=>({...i}));
            const seed=hash(key),role=classify(area,building);
            const result=reflow(spec,theme.id,seed);
            spec.items=result.items;
            spec.__lq53={key,theme,role,seed,moved:result.moved,curated:result.curated,reason:result.reason||'',area,building};
            counts.prepared++;counts.reflowed+=result.moved;counts.kept+=result.curated?1:0;
            counts.byTheme[theme.id]=(counts.byTheme[theme.id]||0)+1;
            return spec.__lq53;
          }
          const api={prepare,drawBack,drawFront,THEMES,classify,get counts(){return {...counts,byTheme:{...counts.byTheme}}},
            describe:(scene)=>{const d=scene?.inside?.spec?.__lq53;return d?{map:d.area,building:d.building,style:d.theme.id,role:d.role,layoutMoves:d.moved,layoutPreserved:d.curated,problem:d.reason}:null;},
            // Development diagnostics: evidence from the actual registered building and interior definitions.
            diagnostics:()=>{const rooms=[];
              for(const a of Se||[])for(const b of a.buildings||[]){const spec=_r[b.name];if(!spec)continue;
                const d=prepare(a.id,b.name,spec,0);
                rooms.push({area:a.id,building:b.name,style:d.theme.id,role:d.role,objects:spec.items.length,original:spec.__lq53Source.length,moved:d.moved,curated:d.curated,reason:d.reason});
              }
              return {total:rooms.length,reflowed:rooms.filter(r=>r.moved>0).length,fallbacks:rooms.filter(r=>r.curated).length,byMap:rooms.reduce((a,r)=>{const g=a[r.area]||(a[r.area]={rooms:0,changed:0,curated:0});g.rooms++;g.changed+=(r.moved>0);g.curated+=(r.curated?1:0);return a;},{}),rooms};
            },
            version:'53.0.0'};
          return Object.freeze(api);
        })();

/* LIFEQUEST V56 — ACCESSIBLE ENTRANCES (2026-11-15)

  const registerCount = {added:0,existing:0,names:[]};
  function makeRoom(area,b) {
    const th=themes[area.id]||themes['english-town'];
    const name=String(b.name||'');
    let category='home';
    if(/freight|workshop|forge|mine|smith|metal|mill/i.test(name))category='workshop';
    else if(/granary|store|storage|warehouse|depot|barn/i.test(name))category='store';
    else if(/weav|tailor|cloth|fabric/i.test(name))category='weaver';
    else if(/bak|bread|cafe|tavern|kitchen/i.test(name))category='bakery';
    else if(/shepherd|farm/i.test(name))category='farm';
    const w=(category==='workshop'||category==='store')?13:12;
    const h=(category==='workshop'||category==='bakery')?10:9;

    // Keep all door approach tiles (center two columns, last three rows) free.

    }
    return {w,h,...th,items,spots:{},guests:[[cx,3]],labels:[{x:cx,y:1,text:name.slice(0,36)}]};
  }
  function ensure(area,b) {
    if(!area||!b||!b.name)return false;
    if(_r[b.name])return true;
    _r[b.name]=makeRoom(area,b);registerCount.added++;registerCount.names.push(area.id+' / '+b.name);
    return true;
  }
  function registerAll() {
    for(const a of Se)for(const b of a.buildings||[]){
      if(_r[b.name])registerCount.existing++;else ensure(a,b);
    }
    return registerCount;
  }
  // Only approach from the building's FRONT, never from behind a solid roof.
  // The original trigger was x<14, 0<=front<24; 7 missing rooms also silently
  // failed the _r lookup. These limits allow E at realistic frontage distances.
  function candidate(scene,b) {
    if(!scene||!scene.player||!b)return null;
    const dx=scene.player.x-(b.door+.5)*T;
    const forward=scene.player.y-(b.y+b.h)*T;
    if(!Number.isFinite(dx)||!Number.isFinite(forward)||forward<-8||forward>84||Math.abs(dx)>45)return null;
    // Be more forgiving at the apparent front edge; do not turn the entire road
    // into a door trigger on wide plots.
    const metric=(dx/45)**2 + (Math.max(0,forward)/84)**2;
    if(metric>1.16)return null;
    return {b,dx,forward,score:Math.abs(dx)*1.1+Math.max(0,forward)*.35};
  }
  function find(scene) {
    if(scene?.inside||!scene?.area?.buildings||!scene?.player)return null;
    let best=null;
    for(const b of scene.area.buildings) {
      const c=candidate(scene,b);
      if(c&&(!best||c.score<best.score))best=c;
    }
    if(!best)return null;
    ensure(scene.area,best.b);
    return best.b;
  }
  function nearAuto(scene,b){const c=candidate(scene,b);
    return !!(c&&Math.abs(c.dx)<18&&c.forward>=-6&&c.forward<=26);
  }
  function diagnostics(){const items=[];
    for(const a of Se)for(const b of a.buildings||[])items.push({map:a.id,name:b.name,interior:!!_r[b.name],doorX:(b.door+.5)*T,doorY:(b.y+b.h)*T});
    return {version:'56.0',...registerCount,total:items.length,missing:items.filter(x=>!x.interior),items};
  }
  registerAll();
  return Object.freeze({ensure,find,nearAuto,candidate,diagnostics,version:'56.0'});
})();

function drawLiving(c){const {W,H,R,L,p,O,E,T,border,h,spar,glow}=c; // Carved cross-section of a living tree (irregular elliptical negative space).

 for(let side of [-1,1])for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const x=side===-1?14+j*10:W-14-j*10, y=86+j*(H-148)/4;L(x,58,x+side*(27+j*3),y,0x2e4b34,.82,9-j);E(x+side*(27+j*3),y,31+j*5,12,0xb6bd85,.27);}

}

function roleFocal(c){const {W,H,R,L,p,O,E,T,border,d,glow}=c,role=d.role||'',id=c.id,cx=W/2;
  // Architectural focal pieces are painted into the wall/background and do not create invisible walls.
  if(id==='royal'){
    if(role==='opera'){R(cx-100,66,200,54,0x403043);R(cx-93,70,186,43,0xc1957e);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){T(cx-95+i*16,66,cx-82+i*16,66,cx-95+i*16,114,0xa54f6a,.73);T(cx+95-i*16,66,cx+82-i*16,66,cx+95-i*16,114,0xa54f6a,.73)}R(cx-72,91,144,15,0x744759);R(cx-68,87,136,3,0xf3d294,.65);}
    else if(role==='throne'||role==='salon'){R(cx-48,70,96,62,0x574054);R(cx-38,76,76,48,0xd4a26d);R(cx-27,72,54,19,0xa1466c);R(cx-20,91,40,33,0x86384f);R(cx-34,122,68,6,0xefcd8d);for(let x of [cx-42,cx+37]){R(x,81,6,48,0xeac88c)}O(cx,83,8,0xffeaa6)}
    else if(role==='academy'){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){R(22+i*(W-45)/4,68,52,57,0x624d57);R(26+i*(W-45)/4,72,44,48,0x9e796d);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)R(29+i*(W-45)/4+j*8,79,5,31,[0xf1d89b,0x91b4bf,0xc79e9e][j%3])}}
  }
  if(id==='living'){
    if(role==='elders'||role==='council'){E(cx,H*.30,134,70,0x2b5e40,.77);E(cx,H*.29,104,48,0xa7be79,.64);E(cx,H*.29,60,20,0xe0d399,.38);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){let a=i*Math.PI/4;O(cx+Math.cos(a)*78,H*.30+Math.sin(a)*40,5,0xd0c892,.65)}}
    else if(role==='root-home'||role==='talori-home'){for(let x of [W*.17,W*.83]){E(x,97,73,49,0x3d6747);E(x,95,55,35,0x8eac79,.72);outlineLeaves(c,x,80,5)} }
    else if(role==='market'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let x=W*(i+.5)/3;E(x,93,W*.27,48,[0x88a868,0xaaa36d,0x75a281][i],.74);R(x-W*.105,110,W*.21,7,0x5f5136);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)O(x-23+j*15,105,7,[0xe5bb6b,0xa8b56d,0xe39470][(i+j)%3])}}
  }
  if(id==='industrial'){
    if(role==='station'){R(cx-57,70,114,61,0x254049);R(cx-51,75,102,48,0x78afb0);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){R(cx-44+i*24,80,20,35,0x29465d);R(cx-40+i*24,84,12,5,0xd9e5ca)}R(cx-64,124,128,6,0xcea86b)}
    else {R(cx-50,69,100,45,0x354957);R(cx-42,74,84,30,0x172e38);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){R(cx-33+j*14,79,8,16,j%2?0xf3b16b:0x76e4d8)}R(cx-47,107,94,5,0xb9a777);}
  }
  if(id==='tech'){R(cx-45,71,90,46,0x173d55);R(cx-39,77,78,33,0x58afbf,.68);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)R(cx-32,82+i*5,Math.max(10,52-i*8),2,i%2?0xfff7ca:0x76f9e0,.8);R(cx-15,113,30,6,0xb5ffec)}
  if(id==='bazaar'){
    if(['clockmaker','jewels','tailor','pottery','metal','food','archive','warehouse','exchange','lord'].includes(role)){
      R(cx-67,66,134,56,0x4b3446,.81);R(cx-62,70,124,48,0xae826b);R(cx-58,73,116,42,0x523e4d);
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++){

        if(role==='clockmaker'){O(x,91,10,0xe9c88e);O(x,91,6,0x56404b);L(x,91,x+4,87,0xffebba,.93,2);}

        else if(role==='pottery'){E(x,90,15,18,0xc68762);R(x-5,78,10,3,0xf9d0a0);}

      }
    }
  }
}
function drawBack(g,s){if(!s.__lq53)return;const c=ctx(g,s);base(c);
 switch(c.id){case 'civic':drawCivic(c);break;case 'industrial':drawIndustrial(c);break;case 'heritage':drawHeritage(c);break;case 'ranger':drawHeritage(c,true);break;case 'atelier':drawAtelier(c);break;case 'tech':drawTech(c);break;case 'royal':drawRoyal(c);break;case 'living':drawLiving(c);break;case 'bazaar':drawBazaar(c);break;}
 roleFocal(c);
 // Entrance reads as an actual open threshold, never a giant invisible barrier.
 const {W,H,p,R,border,L}=c,cx=W/2;R(0,0,6,H,p.edge);R(W-6,0,6,H,p.edge);R(0,H-7,cx-36,7,p.edge);R(cx+36,H-7,W-cx-36,7,p.edge);R(cx-32,H-33,64,33,p.edge,.72);R(cx-26,H-28,52,28,mix(p.floor,p.dark,.26));R(cx-29,H-32,58,5,p.lit,.8);R(cx-12,H-9,24,3,p.accent,.90);}
const itemMap=new Set(['table','counter','desk','schooldesk','chair','sofa','bed','hospbed','shelf','goods','plant','rug','mat','paint','poster','board','screen','glass','glasscase','mirror','clock','flag','lantern','crate','barrel','locker','wardrobe','pew','seat','stove','fireplace','forge','anvil','crystal','orepile','minecart','piano','altar','cakecase','partition','stairs','menu','rack']);
function drawItem(g,it,s){if(!s.__lq53||!itemMap.has(it.k))return false;const c=ctx(g,s),{p,R,L,O,E,T,border,h}=c;const x=it.x*32,y=it.y*32,w=it.w*32,hh=it.h*32,k=it.k,cen=x+w/2,cy=y+hh/2,accent=it.c??p.accent;
 const drop=(xx=x+2,yy=y+4,ww=w-3,hh2=hh-4)=>R(xx+2,yy+3,ww,hh2,p.edge,.25);
 const plank=(xx,yy,ww,hh2,col)=>{R(xx+2,yy+5,ww,hh2,p.edge,.25);R(xx,yy,ww,hh2,col);R(xx,yy,ww,3,mix(col,p.lit,.52));R(xx,yy+hh2-4,ww,4,mix(col,p.edge,.43));border(xx,yy,ww,hh2,p.edge,2,.79);};
 const illumin=(xx,yy,rad,col)=>{O(xx,yy,rad,col,.05);O(xx,yy,rad*.45,col,.12)};
 if(['rug','mat'].includes(k)){carpet(c,x+2,y+1,w-4,hh-2,k==='mat'?mix(p.mat,p.accent,.25):mix(accent,p.mat,.47),k==='rug');return true;}
 if(['table','counter','desk','schooldesk'].includes(k)){drop();R(x+5,y+hh-8,7,8,p.dark);R(x+w-12,y+hh-8,7,8,p.dark);plank(x+2,y+3,w-4,Math.max(14,hh-11),mix(accent,p.wall2,.39));
   if(k==='desk'||k==='schooldesk'){R(x+w*.36,y+6,Math.min(32,w*.4),17,p.edge);R(x+w*.36+3,y+8,Math.min(26,w*.4-6),11,c.id==='tech'?0x54d7e9:0x8bb0b6);R(x+w*.52,y+23,4,5,p.edge);R(x+10,y+hh-16,23,12,p.lit,.82);L(x+12,y+hh-12,x+30,y+hh-12,p.dark,.49,1)}
   else if(k==='counter'){for(let i=1;i<Math.floor(w/26);i++){R(x+i*26,y+hh-10,2,9,p.lit,.48)}R(x+10,y+10,w-20,6,p.lit,.31);for(let i=0;i<Math.min(5,w/28);i++)O(x+14+i*24,y+hh-16,4,[0xe9be75,0xa0d5b0,0xda8da0][i%3]);}
   else {R(x+8,y+10,19,14,0xf1dfbf);R(x+11,y+13,13,2,p.dark,.31);O(x+w-18,y+12,8,0xf4e6c7);O(x+w-18,y+12,5,0x956a55);R(x+9,y+hh-16,14,4,mix(p.accent,p.lit,.4));}return true;}
 if(k==='chair'){R(x+7,y+4,w-14,11,p.dark);R(x+9,y+5,w-18,8,mix(accent,p.wall2,.20));R(x+7,y+17,w-14,hh-23,p.edge);R(x+10,y+18,w-20,Math.max(5,hh-27),mix(accent,p.lit,.13));R(x+9,y+hh-6,4,5,p.dark);R(x+w-13,y+hh-6,4,5,p.dark);return true;}

function atmosphere(c){
 const {W,H,r,l,t,e,o,p,s,style,rnd}=c;
 // Native pixel geometry, not canvas filters: directional light beams fall across
 // existing floor tiles, varying by cultural setting and the room's occupation.
 let tint={living:0xe1f2a5,royal:0xffd69e,tech:0x8af4f5,industrial:0xf4ba83,heritage:0xffcf94,ranger:0xc2e5af,civic:0xffd5a6,atelier:0xffddb6,bazaar:0xffd199}[style]||p.glow;
 const ww=W*.24;
 for(let k=0;k<2;k++){
  let sx=style==='living'?W*(k?.64:.36):W*(k?.76:.24), dx=(k?1:-1)*Math.min(W*.2,140);
  t(sx-27,68,sx+22,68,sx+dx+57,Math.min(H-32,190),tint,.045);
  t(sx-27,68,sx+dx+57,Math.min(H-32,190),sx+dx-48,Math.min(H-32,190),tint,.033);
  e(sx+dx,Math.min(H-32,190),W*.19,22,tint,.04);
 }
 // Shadow under any real fixture: tells the player where the furniture actually sits.
 for(const it of s.items||[]){
  if(['rug','mat','paint','poster','clock','flag','glass','mirror','board','screen','menu','stairs','portal','lantern'].includes(it.k))continue;
  const x=it.x*32,y=it.y*32,w=it.w*32,h=it.h*32;
  if(w<=0||h<=0)continue;
  e(x+w*.57+5,y+h*.72+7,Math.max(11,w*.85),Math.max(6,h*.42),p.ink,.16);
  r(x+5,y+h*.82+6,Math.max(3,w-10),5,p.ink,.095);
 }
 // Cloth seams, timber dowels, mineral scratches, carved motifs: small but not noise.
 for(let i=0;i<Math.min(62,Math.floor(W*H/4400));i++){
  let xx=18+rnd(80+i)*(W-36),yy=85+rnd(230+i)*(H-124);

  else if(style==='living'){e(xx,yy,9,4,i%2?0x9fbe8a:0x557853,.18)}
  else if(style==='industrial'){r(xx,yy,8,2,i%2?0xb0b5ad:0x1c323d,.12)}
  else if(style==='tech'){r(xx,yy,6,1,p.glow,.16)}
  else if(style==='atelier'){r(xx,yy,3+(i%4),2+(i%3),[0xdbac8f,0x73c6b8,0x9c9fcf][i%3],.25)}
  else if(style==='bazaar'){r(xx,yy,7,1,0xeec18b,.15)}
 }
}
function signatureObject(c,it){const {p,r,l,o,e,t,style,d}=c;const x=it.x*32,y=it.y*32,w=it.w*32,h=it.h*32,cx=x+w/2,cy=y+h/2,role=d.role||'';

  }

// V55 ROOM DENSITY PASS: physical props, never visual-only solid objects.
// Validate the whole walkable graph before installing them in the real room spec.
const enrichStats={rooms:0,added:0,kept:0};
function enrichRoom(spec){
 if(!spec||spec.__lq55Enriched)return 0;
 spec.__lq55Enriched=true;enrichStats.rooms++;
 const d=spec.__lq53;if(!d||!Array.isArray(spec.items)||spec.w<10||spec.h<9){enrichStats.kept++;return 0}
 const W=spec.w,H=spec.h,cx=Math.floor(W/2),role=d.role||'',style=d.theme?.id||'civic';
 const noBlock=new Set(['rug','mat','paint','poster','flag','glass','mirror','board','menu','screen','clock','stairs','portal','lantern']);
 const real=new Set(['plant','barrel','crate','rack','shelf','chair','goods','locker','wardrobe','glasscase','cakecase']);
 const used=Array.from({length:H},()=>Array(W).fill(false));
 const occup=(it,mark)=>{if(noBlock.has(it.k))return;for(let y=Math.max(0,it.y|0);y<Math.min(H,it.y+it.h);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,it.x|0);x<Math.min(W,it.x+it.w);x++)used[y][x]=mark};
 spec.items.forEach(it=>occup(it,true));
 const occupied=(x,y)=>x<1||x>=W-1||y<2||y>=H-1||used[y][x];
 const reachCheck=()=>{
  let start=[cx,H-2],seen=new Set(),todo=[start];
  if(occupied(...start))return false;
  const key=(x,y)=>x+','+y;seen.add(key(...start));
  for(let i=0;i<todo.length;i++){const [x,y]=todo[i];for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){const a=x+dx,b=y+dy,k=key(a,b);if(!seen.has(k)&&!occupied(a,b)){seen.add(k);todo.push([a,b])}}}
  // Preserve visits to guests and NPC placements if they were reachable beforehand.
  for(const pos of [...Object.values(spec.spots||{}),...(spec.guests||[])]){
   if(!Array.isArray(pos))continue;const a=pos[0]|0,b=pos[1]|0;
   if(!occupied(a,b)&&!seen.has(key(a,b)))return false;
  }
  // Ensure every preexisting physical fixture has an accessible neighboring cell.
  for(const it of spec.items){if(noBlock.has(it.k))continue;let ok=false;
   for(let y=it.y-1;y<=it.y+it.h;y++)for(let x=it.x-1;x<=it.x+it.w;x++){
    if(x>=it.x&&x<it.x+it.w&&y>=it.y&&y<it.y+it.h)continue;
    if(seen.has(key(x,y))){ok=true;break;}
   }
   if(!ok)return false;
  }
  return true;
 };
 // Do not change a room with an already unconnected design.
 if(!reachCheck()){enrichStats.kept++;return 0;}
 const recipe={
   living:['plant','plant','crate','lantern'],
   bazaar:['goods','crate','barrel','plant'],
   royal:['plant','lantern','plant','chair'],
   industrial:['barrel','crate','rack','barrel'],
   tech:['plant','locker','chair','lantern'],
   atelier:['plant','crate','chair','plant'],
   heritage:['crate','barrel','chair','lantern'],
   ranger:['crate','plant','barrel','plant'],
   civic:['plant','chair','crate','plant']
 }[style]||['plant','crate'];
 // Physical prop arrangement along the real side bays, not in the entrance axis.
 const spots=[];
 for(let y=3;y<H-3;y++){for(const x of [1,W-2,2,W-3,3,W-4]){
  if(x>=cx-2&&x<=cx+2&&y>H-6)continue;
  spots.push({x,y,score:Math.abs(x-cx)*.6+Math.abs(y-H*.55)*.4});
 }}
 spots.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
 let added=0;
 for(let n=0;n<Math.min(recipe.length,6);n++){
  const k=recipe[n],obj={k,x:0,y:0,w:1,h:1,c:undefined};let placed=false;
  for(let i=n%2;i<spots.length;i++){
   const {x,y}=spots[i];if(occupied(x,y))continue;
   // Don't crowd existing furniture; leave at least 1 tile between two new solids.
   if([[0,1],[0,-1],[1,0],[-1,0]].some(([dx,dy])=>occupied(x+dx,y+dy)))continue;
   obj.x=x;obj.y=y;occup(obj,true);
   if(reachCheck()){spec.items.push({...obj});added++;placed=true;break;}
   occup(obj,false);
  }
  if(!placed)continue;
 }
 enrichStats.added+=added;if(!added)enrichStats.kept++;return added;
}
function drawBack(g,s){if(!s.__lq53)return;const c=context(g,s);LQ54.drawBack(g,s);floor(c);backdrop(c);atmosphere(c)}
function drawItem(g,it,s){if(!s.__lq53)return LQ54.drawItem(g,it,s);let did=LQ54.drawItem(g,it,s);const c=context(g,s);moreFurniture(c,it);signatureObject(c,it);return did;}
function drawFront(g,s){if(!s.__lq53)return;LQ54.drawFront(g,s);const c=context(g,s),{W,H,r,o,p,d,style}=c;
 // Delicate screen-space highlights, attached to architecture only.
 for(let x=15;x<W-13;x+=84){r(x,55,38,3,p.hi,.34);r(x+5,58,27,2,p.ink,.29)}
 if(style==='living'){for(let x=38;x<W-18;x+=93){leaf(c,x,20,.55)}}
 if(style==='royal'){for(let x=46;x<W-28;x+=117)lamp(c,x,59)}
 if(style==='tech'){for(let x=44;x<W-24;x+=110)r(x,60,65,2,0x7df7e7,.51)}
}
return Object.freeze({drawBack,drawItem,drawFront,enrichRoom,get stats(){return {...enrichStats}},version:'55.0.0',context,palettes});
})();
window.LQInteriorV55 = LQ55;

const LQ57 = (() => {

 const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));

   if(!g.v57||typeof g.v57!=='object')g.v57={version:1,completed:{},interactions:0,rooms:{}};

 function roleFor(area,building,role){

  }else if(d.style==='bazaar'){
   // Merchant guild architecture, trading counters and vaulted display frontage.
   r(9,8,w-18,44,dark);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let x=27+i*(w-54)/4;r(x-6,12,12,42,wood);r(x-4,8,8,4,cream);r(x-3,17,6,27,light,.30)}
   if(d.role==='clockmaker'){for(let i=0;i<5;i++)clock(cx-102+i*50,29,i===2?23:15);
    r(cx-118,59,236,6,wood);r(cx-114,60,228,2,cream,.6)}
   else {for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const x=cx-105+i*35;r(x-11,18,24,33,wood);r(x-9,20,20,26,mid);r(x-6,23,14,17,light,.24);oval(x+2,29,9,12,[cream,mid,light][i%3],.75)}}
   for(let side of [12,w-44]){r(side,74,30,42,wood,.49);r(side+3,77,24,4,cream,.52)}
  }else if(d.style==='tech'){
   for(let x=16;x<w-40;x+=94){r(x,8,86,44,dark);r(x+3,12,80,35,wood);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)r(x+9,17+i*6,58-(i%2)*16,3,i%2?cream:mid,.83)}
   oval(cx,40,114,39,wood,.6);oval(cx,40,91,25,mid,.66);circ(cx,40,11,light,.8);
   for(let y=93;y<h-60;y+=36){r(cx-12,y,24,2,cream,.31);r(cx-4,y+5,8,8,light,.43)}
  }else if(d.style==='atelier'){
   for(let x=22,i=0;x<w-28;x+=69,i++){r(x,8,62,45,dark);r(x+4,12,54,37,[0xe5a7bc,0x95cfde,0xedd498,0xb2a7ed][i%4]);
    for(let j=0;j<5;j++)r(x+10+j*8,19+j%3*6,7,6,[cream,light,dark][j%3],.57)}
   for(let y=94;y<h-57;y+=58){r(cx-7,y,14,31,cream,.19)}
  }else{
   // Human-scale civic, historic village, forest cabins all use distinct supports.
   for(let x=22;x<w-25;x+=88){shutter(x,10,49);r(x-13,48,70,7,wood,.65)}
   for(let x=14;x<w-10;x+=105){stud(x,4,8,67);}
   if(d.style==='heritage'||d.style==='ranger')for(let x=38;x<w;x+=87){ln(x,0,x+45,60,wood,.74,5)}
   if(d.style==='civic')for(let y=100;y<h-85;y+=74){filigree(cx,y,cream)}
  }
  // Finishing state is visible in the actual room texture and survives save/load.
  if(d.complete){
   for(let j=-2;j<=2;j++){const xx=cx+j*26;r(xx,75,18,8,light,.62);r(xx+4,77,10,3,cream,.9);}
   r(cx-62,79,124,3,cream,.62);
  }
  // Side floor borders and tiled entrance line remain 100% non-solid decoration.
  r(7,82,3,h-129,dark,.32);r(w-10,82,3,h-129,dark,.32);
  for(let y=109;y<h-75;y+=49){r(11,y,13,2,cream,.24);r(w-24,y,13,2,cream,.24)}
  return {role:d.role,style:d.style};
 }
 function scenario(d){
  const a=d.area,role=d.role;
